## Phase 5, Slice 5.1 -- Invoice (Draft, Finalize, Void)

```text
Date: 2026-08-13
Status: Implementation complete, UI verification pending -- backend and frontend both fully built and the backend is fully tested (9 new integration tests, 3 new PDF-renderer unit tests, all passing against the real dev database); the frontend has not yet had a real browser pass. Do not mark this slice's UI as done until a browser pass confirms: (1) the Raise Invoice wiring from the Payment Alert tab; (2) the invoice-add form workflow (GST-registration selection, dynamic line items, submit); (3) the draft-edit/finalize/void workflow on the detail page; (4) PDF download; (5) the project Invoices tab and global /invoices list; (6) responsive layout; (7) keyboard/focus behaviour; (8) zero console errors.
Phase: Phase 5 -- Finance income vertical slice (Slice 5.1 of an undetermined total -- the first slice, chosen because it unblocks the already-stubbed "Raise Invoice" button Payment Alerts (4.3) shipped and closes ADR-064's own disclosed gap: PaymentAlertStatus had no CLOSED value because "nothing in [4.3] can close an alert -- that requires Phase 5's finalized-invoice event.")
Task: Invoice draft/finalize/void, the next unblocking link in docs/04 §11's full chain (Milestone/Installment -> Alert -> Invoice -> Receipt -> Deductions -> Journal -> Bank Match) -- that whole chain is too large for one slice, so this slice is Invoice only. docs/04 §11.3/§11.4/§11.5, docs/06's "Finalize client invoice" table, docs/01's "Milestone, invoice, and receipt" section, and docs/03's invoice invariants are the frozen references. Confirmed with the user via AskUserQuestion before implementation: build real PDF generation now via pdfkit, rather than deferring it -- docs/04 names PDF generation as this screen's own contract. Full rationale in docs/12 ADR-068.
Schema: New Invoice/InvoiceLine models, InvoiceStatus enum (DRAFT/FINALIZED/VOID). InstallmentStatus gains INVOICE_DRAFT/INVOICED; PaymentAlertStatus gains CLOSED. PaymentAlert gains an invoiceId back-reference (the invoice currently closing it, cleared on void-of-finalized). Invoice gains its own paymentAlertId (the alert it was raised from, set once, immutable) -- a separate fact from PaymentAlert.invoiceId, modeled as two distinct named relations between the same two tables; this scalar was caught missing during service implementation (the original schema pass only added the inverse relation) and fixed via a small additive follow-up migration before any test ran. Three hand-added partial unique indexes enforce "at most one active (non-void) invoice per source" and "invoice number unique once assigned, scoped to (org, GST registration, financial year)."
Backend: InvoicesService.getDraftDefaults/createDraftFromAlert/updateDraft/finalize/void/list/getDetail/getPdf. createDraftFromAlert is the only creation path (rejects if the alert is already CLOSED or an active invoice already exists for its source, defense-in-depth ahead of the DB constraint). finalize: validates non-empty lines, enforces the CAPEX milestone approved-amount cap (summing this invoice's taxable amount against every other FINALIZED invoice under the same milestone's events), resolves the covering financial year and required ledger accounts, computes CGST+SGST (same state) or IGST (different state) per line via Prisma.Decimal, calls the existing PostingService.postJournal (sequential, not single-transaction, with a deterministically-derived journal idempotency key -- the same disclosed ADR-066/067 pattern), then in a separate MAX_NUMBER_ATTEMPTS-bounded retry-on-collision transaction assigns the server-generated invoice number, snapshots seller/buyer, flips status, closes the alert, and flips the OPEX installment to INVOICED. void: a DRAFT is a simple status flip; a FINALIZED invoice calls the existing PostingService.reverseJournal (Slice 4.6, its first real caller outside its own tests) before flipping status, reopening the alert, and reverting the installment to PLANNED. Read methods (list/getDetail/getPdf/getDraftDefaults) reuse project.read_all-driven PM-scoping exactly like PaymentAlertsService/MilestonesService already do, piggybacking on ProjectsService.list/getDetail rather than reimplementing scope logic. Six new permissions (invoice.read/create/update/finalize/void/export) mirroring the milestone.*/payment_alert.*/security_instrument.* grant pattern. Three new ledger accounts (OUTPUT_CGST_PAYABLE/OUTPUT_SGST_PAYABLE/OUTPUT_IGST_PAYABLE) under the existing TAX_PAYABLE group. invoice-pdf.util.ts is a pure pdfkit rendering function, no DB access, unit-tested in isolation.
Frontend: the Payment Alert tab's disabled "Raise Invoice" button now navigates to /projects/[id]/invoices/new?paymentAlertId=..., gated by invoice.create. Two new dedicated full-page routes (not a Drawer, per docs/16's "Financial document form" reference): /projects/[id]/invoices/new (draft creation form, GST-registration select, Ant Form.List line-item editor with no client-side tax computation -- CLAUDE.md: "Do not calculate accounting totals independently in React components," authoritative figures only ever come from the server after submit) and /projects/[id]/invoices/[invoiceId] (branches between an editable draft form with Save/Finalize/Void actions, and a read-only finalized/void snapshot with seller/buyer/line/tax-breakdown display, Download PDF, and Void). A new read-only project "Invoices" tab and a new global /invoices list (mirroring the EMD/SD global-list-plus-project-tab shape) -- neither carries an Add Invoice button, since creation is alert-only. New InvoiceIcon and "Invoices" nav item under Finance.
Files changed: packages/database/prisma/schema.prisma, packages/database/prisma/migrations/{20260813120000_phase5_01_invoice, 20260813130000_phase5_01_invoice_payment_alert_origin, 20260813140000_phase5_01_fix_payment_alert_closed_check} (all new), packages/database/prisma/seed.ts (six permissions, three ledger accounts, one entity type), apps/api/src/invoices/{invoices.service.ts, invoices.module.ts, invoice-pdf.util.ts, invoices.integration-spec.ts, invoice-pdf.util.spec.ts} (all new), packages/contracts/src/{ports/invoice.port.ts, routers/invoices.ts} (new) wired into context.ts/router.ts/ports/index.ts/index.ts, apps/api/src/{app.module.ts, main.ts}, apps/api/src/rbac/roles.integration-spec.ts (permission count fix), apps/api/package.json (pdfkit/@types/pdfkit), apps/web/src/app/(platform)/invoices/{page.tsx, shared.tsx} (new), apps/web/src/app/(platform)/projects/[id]/{invoice-tab.tsx, invoices/new/page.tsx, invoices/[invoiceId]/page.tsx} (new), payment-alert-tab.tsx and the project detail page's tab strip updated, packages/ui/src/components/{icons.tsx, nav-items.ts}, docs/11 (Phase 5 sub-phase progress, new), docs/12 (ADR-068).
Migrations: phase5_01_invoice (hand-written, Invoice/InvoiceLine models plus the three partial unique indexes and the InstallmentStatus/PaymentAlertStatus enum extensions), phase5_01_invoice_payment_alert_origin (small additive follow-up restoring the missing Invoice.paymentAlertId scalar+relation), phase5_01_fix_payment_alert_closed_check (fixes a real bug: the pre-existing chk_payment_alerts_snoozed_fields_consistent CHECK constraint from ADR-064's original 4.3 migration only allowed status IN ('OPEN','SNOOZED'), predating CLOSED -- InvoicesService.finalize's status:'CLOSED' update would have failed in production exactly as it failed in this slice's own first integration-test run; this is the finding that caught it, not a code review). All applied via psql + prisma migrate resolve --applied per the established procedure.
Tests: 9 new tests in invoices.integration-spec.ts (CAPEX-event draft creation and intra-state CGST+SGST finalize computing the exact docs/06 journal Dr/Cr split; inter-state IGST computation; OPEX-installment draft creation with installment-status flip to INVOICE_DRAFT, seller/buyer snapshot verification, and the INVOICED transition on finalize; duplicate-active-source rejection; draft void freeing its source for a new draft; finalized-invoice void reversing the journal via reverseJournal, reopening the alert, and reverting the installment to PLANNED; CAPEX milestone-cap rejection across two events under one milestone; idempotent-repeat-createDraftFromAlert returns the original record; cross-org isolation) plus 3 new invoice-pdf.util.spec.ts unit tests (finalized rendering, draft rendering with null fields, inter-state rendering) -- all passing against the real dev database. roles.integration-spec.ts catalog count updated 58 -> 64 (confirmed via direct SQL: exactly 6 invoice.* permissions seeded, with Accountant+Admin holding all six, CEO/PM/Admin Staff holding invoice.read only). payment-alerts.integration-spec.ts and milestones.integration-spec.ts re-run to confirm the CHECK-constraint fix caused no regression -- both pass unchanged. @zitaware/database/@zitaware/contracts/@zitaware/ui rebuild cleanly; apps/api/apps/web both typecheck and lint cleanly; apps/web production build succeeds with all four new routes registered (/invoices, /projects/[id]/invoices/new, /projects/[id]/invoices/[invoiceId], plus the existing /projects/[id] gaining the Invoices tab).
Decisions added/changed: ADR-068 (invoice scope boundary and the every-invoice-from-an-alert rule; the source-uniqueness partial indexes; the DRAFT/FINALIZED/VOID status model and the InstallmentStatus/PaymentAlertStatus extensions; the Invoice.paymentAlertId-vs-PaymentAlert.invoiceId two-relation design; server-generated numbering scoped to GST registration + financial year; seller/buyer/tax snapshotting at finalize; the CGST+SGST-vs-IGST computation rule and the no-client-side-tax-preview decision; the sequential postJournal-then-separate-transaction finalize pattern and its deterministic idempotency-key derivation; the CAPEX milestone-cap enforcement; the DRAFT-flip-vs-FINALIZED-reverseJournal void split; the pdfkit on-demand, never-persisted PDF decision; the six new permissions and their grant pattern; the UI route/tab structure).
Known issues: UI-specific requirements (Raise Invoice wiring, the add-form workflow, the draft-edit/finalize/void workflow, PDF download, the project tab and global list, responsive layout, keyboard/focus behavior, zero console errors) could only be verified by static code review and a production build this session, not a live browser pass -- no browser-automation tool was available. The two bugs this slice did catch (the missing paymentAlertId scalar, and the payment_alerts CHECK constraint predating CLOSED) were both caught by typecheck and by the integration test's very first run respectively, not by code review alone -- a reminder that this session's verification depth is real but bounded by what static analysis and a non-browser test run can catch.
Next task: a real browser pass on Slice 5.1's UI, per the checklist above. After that, Slice 5.2 (Receipt, with multi-invoice allocation) is the natural next link in docs/04 §11's chain, needing its own fresh plan-mode pass.
```

## Phase 5, Slice 5.2 -- Client Receipt (multi-invoice allocation, typed deductions, hold/retention release)

```text
Date: 2026-08-14
Status: Implementation complete, UI verification pending -- backend and frontend both fully built and the backend is fully tested (10 new receipt integration tests, 3 new bank-account integration tests, all passing against the real dev database); the frontend has not yet had a real browser pass. Do not mark this slice's UI as done until a browser pass confirms: (1) the Record Payment wiring from the invoice detail page; (2) the multi-row allocation editor and its nested typed-deduction sub-editor; (3) the server-computed preview display; (4) the Reverse and Release actions on the receipt detail page; (5) the Bank Accounts master screen; (6) the project Payment tab and global Payment Integration list; (7) responsive layout; (8) keyboard/focus behaviour; (9) zero console errors.
Phase: Phase 5 -- Finance income vertical slice (Slice 5.2 of an undetermined total -- the second slice, continuing docs/04 §11's chain from Invoice (5.1) toward Receipt).
Task: Client Receipt with multi-invoice allocation, typed deductions (TDS/GST-TDS/hold/retention/penalty), and hold/retention release -- the next unblocking link after Slice 5.1 made invoices finalizable but not yet collectible. docs/04 §11.6 (project-payment-add.html), docs/06's "Record client receipt with deductions" and "Hold/retention release" tables, docs/01's "Receipt" subsection, and docs/03's receipt domain tree/invariants are the frozen references. Two scope-boundary questions were confirmed with the user via AskUserQuestion before implementation: build a real BankAccount master this slice (not a LedgerAccount-reuse shortcut), and bundle Hold/Retention Release into this same slice rather than deferring it. Full rationale in docs/12 ADR-069.
Schema: New BankAccount master (legal-entity-scoped, required ledgerAccountId, folded into the existing LegalEntityService rather than a new module -- a legal-entity sub-resource reusing legal_entity.read/legal_entity.manage exactly like GST Registrations). New ClientReceipt (header, immutable after posting), ReceiptAllocation (one row per invoice, snapshots the invoice's taxable/CGST/SGST/IGST/total at allocation time, stores settledAmount once at post), AllocationDeduction (nine typed deduction kinds), DeductionRelease (a release against a HOLD/RETENTION deduction, supports repeated partial releases). PenaltyCategory gains two nullable ledger-account overrides (acceptedLedgerAccountId/disputedLedgerAccountId) for per-category posting control, falling back to default accounts when unset. Seven new ledger accounts (three GST-TDS receivable accounts, hold/retention/disputed-deduction receivable, a penalty-revenue-deduction account) under one new OTHER_RECEIVABLES asset group.
Backend: ReceiptsService.postReceipt/reverseReceipt/releaseDeduction/getAllocationCandidates/calculatePreview/getOutstandingDeductions/list/getDetail, injecting PrismaService/PostingService/ProjectsService. A private computeReceiptPlan helper (shared by postReceipt and calculatePreview, so a successful preview is guaranteed to post identically) validates every allocated invoice is FINALIZED, the bank account and every invoice share one legal entity, all invoices share one client, sum(cashAllocated) equals actualAmount exactly, and each allocation doesn't exceed its invoice's live-computed outstanding amount -- then resolves each deduction's target ledger account (fixed codes for TDS/GST-TDS/hold/retention, penalty-category override-or-default for accepted/disputed penalty, direct pick for "other") and aggregates one combined journal: Dr Bank + each distinct deduction account; Cr Accounts Receivable. postReceipt posts the journal first (its own committed transaction, the now five-times-established ADR-066/067/068 sequential pattern) then writes the receipt/allocation/deduction rows in a separate transaction. reverseReceipt reuses the existing reverseJournal (Slice 4.6) without mutating allocation/deduction history -- a reversed receipt's allocations are simply excluded from future outstanding computations via a status filter. releaseDeduction posts docs/06's Dr Bank/Cr Hold-or-Retention-Receivable mirror entry, keyed by the release's own new id (not the deduction's id) so repeated partial releases against the same deduction don't collide with postJournal's duplicate-source-event guard. Four new permissions (receipt.read/post/reverse, deduction.release) mirroring the invoice.* grant pattern; read methods reuse project.read_all-driven PM-scoping via ProjectsService exactly like InvoicesService already does.
Frontend: the invoice detail page's FinalizedOrVoidView gains a "Record Payment" action (gated receipt.post, FINALIZED invoices only) navigating to /projects/[id]/receipts/new?invoiceId=..., pre-seeding that invoice as the first allocation row. The add form (dedicated full page, not a Drawer) has a Form.List-based repeatable allocation editor with a nested Form.List typed-deduction sub-editor per row -- per-row conditional fields (penalty category for accepted/disputed penalty, ledger account for "other") extracted into their own small row components, since AntD's Form.useWatch cannot be called inside a .map() callback (a real react-hooks/rules-of-hooks lint failure caught and fixed during this slice, not assumed away). The form shows zero client-computed settlement math -- a Preview button calls calculatePreview and renders the server response verbatim; Post Receipt calls the real mutation directly rather than gating on a separate preview step. A receipt detail page shows the full allocation/deduction breakdown with Reverse and per-deduction Release actions (a Modal). A project "Payment" tab and a global /payment-integration list mirror Invoice's own tab/list shape, neither carrying an "Add Receipt" button since creation is invoice-initiated only. A new /bank-accounts master screen (list + add/edit Drawer) and nav item under "Organization." The Penalty Category master Drawer gains the two new ledger-account fields.
Files changed: packages/database/prisma/schema.prisma, packages/database/prisma/migrations/20260814090000_phase5_02_receipt (new), packages/database/prisma/seed.ts (four permissions, seven ledger accounts, one account group), apps/api/src/organization/legal-entity.service.ts (BankAccount CRUD added), apps/api/src/receipts/{receipts.service.ts, receipts.module.ts, receipts.integration-spec.ts} (all new), packages/contracts/src/{ports/receipt.port.ts, routers/receipts.ts} (new) plus legal-entity.port.ts/legal-entities.ts extended, wired into context.ts/router.ts/ports/index.ts/index.ts/app.module.ts/main.ts, apps/api/src/penalty-categories/penalty-category.service.ts and packages/contracts/src/{ports/penalty-category.port.ts, routers/penalty-categories.ts} (two new fields), apps/api/src/organization/organization.integration-spec.ts (three new BankAccount tests), apps/web/src/app/(platform)/{bank-accounts, payment-integration}/{page.tsx, shared.tsx} (new), apps/web/src/app/(platform)/projects/[id]/{payment-tab.tsx, receipts/new/page.tsx, receipts/[receiptId]/page.tsx} (new), the invoice detail page and project tab strip updated, the penalty-categories master page updated, packages/ui/src/components/{icons.tsx, nav-items.ts} (two new icons/nav items), docs/11 (5.2 sub-phase entry), docs/12 (ADR-069).
Migrations: phase5_02_receipt -- hand-written (SHADOW_DATABASE_URL wasn't loaded in the working shell for prisma migrate diff, so the migration was hand-written directly per the now-standard fallback procedure rather than fighting the tool), applied via psql + prisma migrate resolve --applied. Purely additive: two nullable columns on penalty_categories, four new tables, two new enums, no backfill needed.
Tests: 10 new tests in receipts.integration-spec.ts (single-invoice receipt with a TDS deduction posts the exact docs/06 journal; multi-invoice allocation sums deductions per ledger account across allocations into one combined journal; GST-TDS/hold/retention/accepted-penalty/disputed-penalty deductions all resolve to their correct accounts including a penalty-category override vs the default fallback; over-allocation rejection; sum(cashAllocated) != actualAmount rejection; non-FINALIZED-invoice rejection; reversal reverses the journal and frees the invoice for a second receipt; hold release posts the mirror journal, reduces live-computed outstanding, and rejects an over-release; idempotent-repeat-returns-original; cross-org isolation) plus 3 new bank-account tests appended to organization.integration-spec.ts (create/list/update/deactivate; cross-org ledger-account rejection; two bank accounts under different ledger accounts for one legal entity) -- all passing against the real dev database. @zitaware/database/@zitaware/contracts/@zitaware/ui rebuild cleanly; apps/api/apps/web both typecheck and lint cleanly; apps/web production build succeeds with all six new routes registered (/bank-accounts, /payment-integration, /projects/[id]/receipts/new, /projects/[id]/receipts/[receiptId], plus the existing invoice detail page gaining Record Payment and the project page gaining the Payment tab).
Decisions added/changed: ADR-069 (BankAccount folded into LegalEntityService rather than a new module; the receipt/allocation/deduction/release schema shape and the live-computed-outstanding-not-stored decision; the per-deduction-type ledger-account resolution table including the penalty-category-override-with-default-fallback design; the full-allocation-required v1 simplification and why an unallocated-balance treatment was deliberately not invented; the combined-one-journal-per-receipt posting shape; the release-keyed-by-its-own-id fix enabling repeated partial releases; the four new permissions and PM-scoping reuse; the UI route/tab structure and the no-client-side-settlement-math decision).
Known issues: UI-specific requirements (listed in Status above) could only be verified by static code review and a production build this session, not a live browser pass -- no browser-automation tool was available. Two real bugs were caught and fixed during this slice, both by the integration test suite rather than by typecheck or code review: detailInclude's object-spread of summaryInclude silently discarded the nested allocations.include override needed for clientName resolution (object spread replaces a whole key, it doesn't merge nested objects) -- every getDetail/postReceipt/reverseReceipt call crashed until fixed; and the initial toSummary read invoice.project.name (the project's own name) instead of invoice.project.client.name for the clientName field -- a real display bug that would have shown the project name where the client name belongs on every receipt list row. A third issue (not a bug, a lint-caught constraint) required extracting per-row form components since AntD's Form.useWatch cannot run inside a .map() callback under this repo's react-hooks/rules-of-hooks enforcement.
Next task: a real browser pass on Slice 5.2's UI, per the checklist above. After that, the next link in docs/04 §11's chain is Bank Transaction Import/Reconciliation/Matching (its own separate NEW_REQUIRED screen per docs/04 §18) or Credit/Debit Notes, whichever the user prioritizes -- needs its own fresh plan-mode pass.
```

## Phase 5, Slices 5.1/5.2 fix pass -- Invoice-add and Receipt-add screens vs. their HTML contracts

```text
Date: 2026-08-14
Status: Complete. No schema or business-rule change -- this closes UI-coverage gaps found by re-auditing project-invoice-add.html and project-payment-add.html against docs/04 §11.4/§11.6 and what 5.1/5.2 actually shipped.
Phase: Phase 5 -- Finance income vertical slice (fix pass on already-shipped Slices 5.1 and 5.2, not a new slice).
Task: Re-audited both HTML prototypes, their paired 1440x900 screenshots, and docs/04 §11.4 (MINOR_CHANGE)/§11.6 (FUNCTIONAL_CHANGE) against the live invoices/receipts add and detail pages. Confirmed the prototypes' free-pick Milestone/Installment and Billing-company dropdowns are correctly already removed/restricted per docs/04's own corrections (not gaps), and that live client-side GST/waterfall math is correctly not replicated (CLAUDE.md: never calculate accounting totals independently in React). Found three real gaps and one discoverability gap, confirmed the fix approach for the fourth with the user via AskUserQuestion (keep Receipt-add and Hold/Retention Release as two separate flows, but make Release reachable without already being inside one receipt).
Fixes: (1) wired the existing entity-agnostic DocumentsTab (already used by Lead/Tender/Project) into both the invoice detail page (draft and finalized/void states) and the receipt detail page -- ENTITY_TYPES rows for INVOICE/RECEIPT were seeded in 5.1/5.2 specifically for this but never consumed; required zero backend change since the documents router's entityType is a free string and documentCategoryEntityType is permissive-by-default with no seed rows restricting either category. (2) Invoice.servicePeriodStart/servicePeriodEnd (OPEX only, already on InvoiceDraftDefaults/InvoiceDetailData) now render as a read-only "Service period" line on the invoice add form and on both invoice-detail states -- no port change, just consuming fields that already existed. (3) FinalizedOrVoidView now renders invoice.narrative in a "Note" block when set -- previously the field was only visible while still a draft, so a finalized invoice's own note became permanently invisible. (4) Extracted the receipt detail page's local ReleaseModal into payment-integration/shared.tsx as a reusable component (also exporting a ReleaseDeductionTarget type), then added an "Outstanding Holds & Retentions" table (reusing the existing getOutstandingDeductions/releaseDeduction) to both the project Payment tab (projectId-scoped) and the global Payment Integration list (org-wide) -- Release was previously only reachable from inside one specific receipt's own detail page.
Follow-up (same date, user-requested after the above landed): the global /invoices and /payment-integration list pages had no "Add" entry point at all, unlike every other global list in the app (e.g. EMD/SD's "Add EMD/SD"). Both invoice and receipt creation remain deliberately origin-constrained (docs/04: no ad-hoc invoice creation; a receipt always seeds from one specific FINALIZED invoice) -- rather than inventing a second, unconstrained creation path, both new buttons open a small project-then-source picker modal that resolves to the existing constrained route: RaiseInvoiceModal (apps/web/src/app/(platform)/invoices/shared.tsx) picks a project then one of its open/snoozed payment alerts (reusing paymentAlerts.listForProject, the same query the Payment Alert tab already uses) and navigates to /projects/[id]/invoices/new?paymentAlertId=...; RecordPaymentModal (apps/web/src/app/(platform)/payment-integration/shared.tsx) picks a project then one of its FINALIZED invoices (reusing invoices.list) and navigates to /projects/[id]/receipts/new?invoiceId=.... "Add Invoice" is gated invoice.create, "Record Payment" is gated receipt.post, matching the permission already required on the routes they land on. Zero backend/schema change.
Files changed: apps/web/src/components/documents-tab.tsx (entityType union widened to add INVOICE/RECEIPT), apps/web/src/app/(platform)/projects/[id]/invoices/{new/page.tsx, [invoiceId]/page.tsx} (DocumentsTab card, service-period line, narrative block), apps/web/src/app/(platform)/projects/[id]/receipts/[receiptId]/page.tsx (DocumentsTab card, ReleaseModal now imported from shared instead of a local copy), apps/web/src/app/(platform)/payment-integration/shared.tsx (ReleaseModal + ReleaseDeductionTarget extracted here, plus the new RecordPaymentModal), apps/web/src/app/(platform)/projects/[id]/payment-tab.tsx and apps/web/src/app/(platform)/payment-integration/page.tsx (Outstanding Holds & Retentions card each, page.tsx also gets the Record Payment button/modal), apps/web/src/app/(platform)/invoices/{shared.tsx, page.tsx} (new RaiseInvoiceModal, Add Invoice button).
Migrations: none.
Tests: none added -- this pass touches only frontend consumption of already-tested backend surfaces (documents.linkToEntity, receipts.getOutstandingDeductions, receipts.releaseDeduction), no new business rule. Full apps/web lint, typecheck, and production build all run clean after every fix.
Decisions added/changed: none -- no new ADR; this completes UI coverage already decided in ADR-068/069, it introduces no new business rule or schema.
Known issues: docs/04 §11.5's invoice-list requirement that list columns separately expose cash received and true outstanding (now computable since Receipt data exists) remains a separate, unstarted follow-up -- deliberately out of scope for this targeted fix pass on the two add/detail screens the user flagged. This pass, like 5.1/5.2 themselves, was verified via lint/typecheck/build and static review only -- no live browser pass was performed (no browser-automation tool available).
Next task: a real browser pass covering 5.1, 5.2, and this fix pass together is still outstanding. After that, Bank Transaction Import/Reconciliation/Matching or Credit/Debit Notes remain the next candidates in docs/04 §11's chain, or docs/04 §11.5's list-column follow-up noted above.
```

## Payment Alert header notification bell

```text
Date: 2026-08-18
Status: Complete. No schema change, no new permission, no new ADR.
Phase: Phase 4 (Payment Alerts, 4.3) header-surfacing follow-up -- not a new phase-level slice.
Correction to the session record: automatic PaymentAlert generation was believed missing earlier this session ("no production code path creates one from a due date"). That was wrong -- the search only covered apps/api/src and missed apps/worker/src, where apps/worker/src/payment-alert-opener.ts has implemented this correctly since Phase 4.3 (commit 8338121), running on a 15-minute setInterval plus once on worker boot, per the documented ADR-064 rule (CAPEX opens at dueDate-reminderOffsetDays or on early achievement; OPEX opens at plannedInvoiceDate-reminderOffsetDays; SNOOZED alerts reopen once snoozedUntil passes). No code change was needed for alert generation itself -- only the second half of the ask (surfacing already-open alerts in the app header) was a real gap.
Task: the app header already had a fully-styled but non-functional bell icon (packages/ui/src/components/Topbar.tsx -- hasUnreadNotifications prop, badge markup, BellIcon -- no click handler, and AppShell never even passed the prop through). This slice wires real data into that existing bell rather than building new header chrome.
Backend: one new method on the existing PaymentAlertPort/PaymentAlertsService, getHeaderSummary(organizationId, actorId, canSeeAll) -> { overdueCount, dueCount, items } -- OPEN alerts only (SNOOZED/CLOSED excluded; Snooze remains the "I've seen this" mechanism, no new read/unread concept introduced), items sorted soonest/most-overdue-first and capped at 10 while the counts reflect the true total. Reuses the existing module-level toSummary helper (no duplicated derivation logic) and the exact PM-scoping idiom already established in InvoicesService.list/ReceiptsService.list (only resolves this.projects.list(...) when !canSeeAll). New router procedure on the existing payment-alerts router, gated by the existing payment_alert.read permission -- no new permission needed.
Frontend: Topbar.tsx gains a generic (contracts-agnostic -- packages/ui still has zero dependency on @zitaware/contracts) TopbarNotificationItem shape and wraps the bell button in an AntD Dropdown (popupRender, matching antd 5.29's current non-deprecated prop) mirroring the adjacent user-menu dropdown already in the same file; each item's status renders via the existing StatusTag component (already maps OVERDUE->neg/DUE->warn, already used identically in PaymentAlertTab) rather than a new color scheme. AppShell.tsx's confirmed bug (never forwarding hasUnreadNotifications to Topbar at all) is fixed by threading the three new optional props through. platform-shell-client.tsx (the one real call site of AppShell) fetches the new query with refetchInterval: 60_000 (no polling/live-update infra existed anywhere in apps/web before this), gated on session.permissions.includes('payment_alert.read') so ungated roles never fire the request. A clicked alert navigates to /projects/[id]?tab=payment-alert -- projects/[id]/page.tsx previously had no tab deep-linking at all (fully uncontrolled AntD Tabs); it's now driven by useSearchParams with a defensive clamp to a known tab-key set, which per Next.js convention required wrapping the page in Suspense (matching the same pattern already used by invoices/new/page.tsx for its own useSearchParams call).
Files changed: packages/contracts/src/ports/payment-alert.port.ts (+ports/index.ts, +index.ts re-exports), apps/api/src/payment-alerts/payment-alerts.service.ts, packages/contracts/src/routers/payment-alerts.ts, apps/api/src/payment-alerts/payment-alerts.integration-spec.ts (5 new tests in a dedicated nested describe with its own project/milestone fixtures, avoiding order-coupling with the file's shared fixtures), packages/ui/src/components/{Topbar.tsx, Topbar.test.tsx, AppShell.tsx, index.ts}, apps/web/src/app/(platform)/platform-shell-client.tsx, apps/web/src/app/(platform)/projects/[id]/page.tsx.
Migrations: none.
Tests: 5 new payment-alerts integration tests (excludes SNOOZED and computes projectName/counts correctly; sorts ascending by due date; caps items at 10 while overdueCount reflects the true total of 11; cross-org isolation; PM-scoping including the no-Employee-record fallback returning an empty result rather than throwing) -- full apps/api integration suite re-run clean (23 files, 247 tests, zero regressions). 4 new Topbar.test.tsx cases (empty state, item render + click, overflow footer shown/hidden) -- full packages/ui suite re-run clean (4 files, 23 tests). apps/web lint/typecheck/production build all clean; apps/api lint/typecheck/unit-test all clean.
Decisions added/changed: none -- no new ADR; additive UI plus one new read query, no business-rule change. The session-record correction above is the only thing worth remembering: Phase 4.3's alert generation was already complete before this slice started.
Known issues: no live browser pass performed this session (no browser-automation tool available) -- the manual click-through checklist (badge/dropdown rendering, click-to-tab navigation, Snooze removing an item within the poll interval, the 10-item cap with correct overflow count, permission-gated inert state, PM-scoping, mobile Drawer layout, direct-URL and invalid-tab-value deep-linking) is still outstanding. A dedicated global "all payment alerts across the org" list page (mirroring /invoices, /payment-integration) remains a deliberately out-of-scope future follow-up -- the header dropdown was judged sufficient for what was asked.
Next task: the outstanding live-browser-verification backlog (now spanning 5.1, 5.2, the 5.1/5.2 fix pass, and this header-bell slice) is the natural next checkpoint before further net-new feature work.
```

## Invoice list -- live outstanding amount + richer Record Payment dropdown

```text
Date: 2026-08-19
Status: Complete. No schema change, no new permission, no new ADR.
Phase: Phase 5 (Invoice, Slice 5.1) polish -- closes part of docs/04 SS11.5's already-disclosed gap ("list columns separately expose cash received and true outstanding"), named as an unstarted follow-up in the 5.1/5.2 fix pass entry above.
Task: RecordPaymentModal's "Finalized invoice" dropdown (used both from the global Payment Integration list and from a project's own Payment tab) only showed the invoice number and its total amount, giving no sense of the milestone it belongs to or how much of it is actually still owed. sourceTitle (the milestone/installment short name) already existed on InvoiceSummary; outstandingAmount did not.
Backend: InvoiceSummary gains outstandingAmount: string | null. toSummary(row, settledAmount = 0) in apps/api/src/invoices/invoices.service.ts takes an optional settledAmount parameter (backward-compatible default, since toSummary is called from 5 other sites -- createDraftFromAlert/updateDraft/finalize/void/toDetail -- none of which needed to change) and computes totalAmount - settledAmount. InvoicesService.list batches one receiptAllocation.groupBy({ by: ['invoiceId'], where: { invoiceId: { in: rows }, receipt: { organizationId, status: { not: 'REVERSED' } } }, _sum: { settledAmount: true } }) rather than querying per row -- the same live-computed-never-stored pattern already established by ReceiptsService.getAllocationCandidates/getOutstandingDeductions.
Frontend: apps/web/src/app/(platform)/invoices/shared.tsx's InvoiceRow gains the matching field; RecordPaymentModal's dropdown label changed from `${invoiceNumber} -- ${formatMoney(totalAmount)}` to `${invoiceNumber} -- ${sourceTitle} -- Total ${formatMoney(totalAmount)} -- Due ${formatMoney(outstandingAmount)}` (reads 0 once an invoice is fully settled, per the request).
Files changed: packages/contracts/src/ports/invoice.port.ts, apps/api/src/invoices/invoices.service.ts, apps/api/src/receipts/receipts.integration-spec.ts, apps/web/src/app/(platform)/invoices/shared.tsx, apps/web/src/app/(platform)/payment-integration/shared.tsx.
Migrations: none.
Tests: one new test added to receipts.integration-spec.ts (InvoicesService.list reports outstandingAmount live, net of settled receipts and excluding reversed ones) added there rather than to invoices.integration-spec.ts, since ReceiptsService's fixtures (bank account, payment mode, finalizeInvoice helper) already exist in that file and InvoicesService is already instantiated there -- asserts full-outstanding on finalize, partial-settlement after a receipt, reversal restoring the full outstanding amount, and 0.00 after full settlement. Full receipts + invoices integration suites re-run clean (20/20). apps/api and apps/web typecheck, lint, and @zitaware/contracts build all clean.
Decisions added/changed: none.
Known issues: none.
Next task: unchanged -- the outstanding live-browser-verification backlog remains the next checkpoint; docs/04 SS11.5's list-column follow-up is now partially closed (outstanding amount surfaced in the Record Payment picker) but the global /invoices list table itself does not yet show a dedicated outstanding column.
```

## Phase 5, Slice 5.3: Credit Note / Debit Note

```text
Date: 2026-08-19
Status: Implementation complete, UI verification pending -- backend fully built and tested (12 new credit-debit-notes integration tests, 1 new outstandingAmount-nets-out-a-credit-note test, all passing against the real dev database); the frontend has not yet had a real browser pass. Do not mark this slice's UI as done until a browser pass confirms: (1) the Issue Credit Note/Debit Note actions and add-form workflow from the invoice detail page; (2) the note detail page's draft-edit/finalize/void flow; (3) the Credit/Debit Notes card on the invoice detail page; (4) the global /credit-debit-notes list and its Add flow; (5) the invoice list's outstanding-amount figure correctly dropping after a credit note and rising after a debit note; (6) responsive layout; (7) keyboard/focus behavior; (8) zero browser-console errors.
Phase: Phase 5 -- Finance income vertical slice (Slice 5.3 of an undetermined total -- the third slice, continuing docs/04 SS11/SS18's chain from Invoice (5.1) and Receipt (5.2) toward the remaining Bank Reconciliation / Ledger Opening Balances items).
Task: Credit Note / Debit Note -- picked as the next strategic Phase 5 task over Bank Transaction Reconciliation (blocked on Ledger Opening Balances not existing yet, per docs/11's own stated dependency) and over Opening Balances itself (zero screen contract or prototype anywhere, the highest-ambiguity remaining option). This closes a real operational gap: the only invoice-correction tool today is Void, which kills the whole invoice and forces a full re-bill -- there was no way to make a partial correction (e.g. a rate/quantity error found after finalization) without destroying and re-raising the whole document. docs/04 SS18 names "Credit Note and Debit Note" as its own NEW_REQUIRED deliverable; docs/06 freezes the Credit Note posting rule ("Reverse revenue/output tax/receivable according to the credited values"); three prior ADRs (067/068/069) all explicitly deferred this as "Phase 5 income-module territory." Confirmed with the user via AskUserQuestion before implementation: build Credit and Debit Note together as one model with a type discriminator (Debit Note posts the mirror direction, since no frozen docs/06 rule exists for it); cap cumulative Credit Notes at the invoice's taxable amount (mirrors the CAPEX-cap precedent), no cap on Debit Notes; a note always posts into today's open accounting period, never the original invoice's own (matches the Reversal Mechanism precedent, ADR-067). Full rationale in docs/12 ADR-070.
Schema: New CreditDebitNote (type/status/reason/noteNumber/noteDate/taxable-cgst-sgst-igst-total amounts/journal linkage/void fields, denormalized invoiceId+projectId+legalEntityId+gstRegistrationId from the source invoice, NOT unique on invoiceId since one invoice may receive many notes over time) and CreditDebitNoteLine (byte-for-byte the same shape as InvoiceLine, free-form lines not tied to specific original InvoiceLine ids). Hand-added partial unique index scoping note numbering to (organizationId, gstRegistrationId, financialYearId, type) so Credit and Debit Notes get independent CN-/DN- series.
Backend: CreditDebitNotesService.createDraft/updateDraft/finalize/void/list/getDetail, injecting PrismaService/PostingService/ProjectsService -- deliberately mirrors InvoicesService's own shape throughout, reusing its exported LEDGER_CODES and computeLines rather than duplicating them. finalize sums the note's lines, applies the CREDIT-only cumulative cap check (same aggregate-then-compare shape as Invoice.finalize's CAPEX cap), resolves the financial year/accounting period from today's date (never the source invoice's own invoiceDate), and posts the journal via the existing PostingService.postJournal (reverseJournal was confirmed unusable here since it only supports full-entry reversal, not a scaled amount) -- Credit Note debits Revenue+GST and credits Accounts Receivable (the literal mirror of Invoice.finalize's own journal); Debit Note posts the identical direction as Invoice.finalize itself. Never touches the source Invoice's own status or PaymentAlert. void mirrors Invoice.void's FINALIZED branch, reusing reverseJournal against the note's own journalEntryId. InvoicesService.list()'s outstandingAmount (shipped the day before this slice) is extended with a second batched creditDebitNote.groupBy so it correctly nets out finalized notes -- a required change, not optional, since it would have silently gone wrong the moment any note was finalized otherwise. Five new permissions (credit_debit_note.read/create/update/finalize/void) mirroring the invoice.* grant pattern; permission catalog count corrected 68 -> 73 (the seed script was re-run against the local dev DB to apply them for real, not just added to the seed.ts source).
Frontend: the invoice detail page's FinalizedOrVoidView gains "Issue Credit Note..."/"Issue Debit Note..." actions (gated credit_debit_note.create, FINALIZED invoices only) navigating to a dedicated add route, plus a new "Credit / Debit Notes" card at the page bottom (sibling to the existing Documents card) listing notes against that invoice. New add page (/projects/[id]/invoices/[invoiceId]/credit-debit-notes/new?type=CREDIT|DEBIT) and detail page (.../credit-debit-notes/[noteId]) reuse LineItemsEditor/gstRegistrationSelectOptions from invoices/shared.tsx verbatim -- simpler than Invoice's own finalize modal since no invoice-date input is needed (finalize always posts today). A new global /credit-debit-notes list mirrors /invoices' own shape, with an IssueCreditDebitNoteModal (project-then-invoice-then-type picker) for its Add entry point -- included now rather than deferred, since Invoice/Receipt's own missing-Add-button gap was flagged as a real usability miss during their own fix pass. New CreditDebitNoteIcon and nav item under "Finance."
Files changed: packages/database/prisma/schema.prisma, packages/database/prisma/migrations/20260819090000_phase5_03_credit_debit_note (new), packages/database/prisma/seed.ts (five permissions + role grants), apps/api/src/invoices/invoices.service.ts (exports LEDGER_CODES/computeLines, extends list()/toSummary()), apps/api/src/credit-debit-notes/{credit-debit-notes.service.ts, credit-debit-notes.module.ts, credit-debit-notes.integration-spec.ts} (all new), packages/contracts/src/{ports/credit-debit-note.port.ts, routers/credit-debit-notes.ts} (new), wired into context.ts/router.ts/ports/index.ts/index.ts/app.module.ts/main.ts, apps/api/src/rbac/roles.integration-spec.ts (catalog count 68 -> 73), apps/api/src/receipts/receipts.integration-spec.ts (new outstandingAmount-nets-out-credit-note test), apps/web/src/app/(platform)/credit-debit-notes/{page.tsx, shared.tsx} (new), apps/web/src/app/(platform)/projects/[id]/invoices/[invoiceId]/credit-debit-notes/{new/page.tsx, [noteId]/page.tsx} (new), the invoice detail page updated, packages/ui/src/components/{icons.tsx, index.ts, nav-items.ts} (new icon + nav item), docs/11 (5.3 sub-phase entry), docs/12 (ADR-070).
Migrations: phase5_03_credit_debit_note -- hand-written, applied via prisma migrate deploy + prisma generate against local Postgres. Purely additive: two new enums, two new tables, no backfill.
Tests: 12 new tests in credit-debit-notes.integration-spec.ts (draft creation rejected against DRAFT/VOID invoices; CGST+SGST vs IGST line computation matches Invoice.finalize's own; CREDIT and DEBIT finalize each post the exact expected journal; CN/DN numbering sequences proven independent via a live before/after count rather than a hardcoded starting number; cumulative-credit-exceeds-invoice-taxable-amount rejection; a large DEBIT note is not capped; a note finalizes successfully even after the source invoice's own accounting period has since been LOCKED, with its journal confirmed to post today not the invoice's date; void reverses the journal; idempotent-repeat-returns-original; cross-org isolation; PM-scoping) plus 1 new test appended to receipts.integration-spec.ts proving InvoicesService.list().outstandingAmount nets out a finalized Credit Note. Three real test-isolation bugs caught and fixed while writing/extending these suites (none a product bug): the period-lock test permanently locked the one shared accounting period every other test's finalizeInvoice() helper depends on, fixed by reordering it to run last; a first failed run's afterAll aborted partway through (missing accountingPeriodTransition cleanup, now fixed) leaving orphaned fixture rows whose GSTINs collided on retry and, once the full suite ran in parallel, also with receipts.integration-spec.ts's own fixture GSTIN -- cleaned up and changed to non-colliding values; and receipts.integration-spec.ts's own afterAll had no creditDebitNote/creditDebitNoteLine cleanup at all, so the new test's rows blocked the subsequent invoice delete under fk_credit_debit_notes_invoice's ON DELETE RESTRICT -- fixed by adding that cleanup. Full apps/api integration suite re-run clean (24 files, 261 tests) after the reseed. @zitaware/contracts/@zitaware/ui rebuild cleanly; apps/api/apps/web both lint clean; apps/web production build succeeds with all three new routes registered (/credit-debit-notes, the project-scoped new/[noteId] routes, plus the existing invoice detail page gaining two actions and a card).
Decisions added/changed: ADR-070 (the combined Credit+Debit Note model with a type discriminator; Debit Note's mirror-posting treatment since no frozen docs/06 rule existed; the credit-cap-enforcement rule; the always-posts-into-today's-open-period rule; free-form lines not tied to original invoice line ids; PDF/export and Documents-tab attachment deferred as disclosed follow-ups; the required InvoicesService.outstandingAmount extension).
Known issues: apps/web's standalone `npm run typecheck` fails on one pre-existing, unrelated, untracked file pair (src/proxy.ts/proxy.test.ts, a session-timeout redirect feature that predates this slice and was never touched by it) -- Next's own build-time type-check, which actually gates deployment, passes clean. No live browser pass was performed this session (no browser-automation tool available) -- see Status above for the specific checklist.
Next task: the outstanding live-browser-verification backlog now spans 5.1, 5.2, the 5.1/5.2 fix pass, the header-bell slice, and this Credit/Debit Note slice -- still the natural next checkpoint before further net-new feature work. After that, Bank Transaction Import/Reconciliation or Ledger Opening Balances remain the next candidates in docs/04 SS11's chain, whichever the user prioritizes.
```

## Phase 5, Slice 5.4: Ledger Account Opening Balances

```text
Date: 2026-08-19
Status: Implementation complete, UI verification pending -- backend fully built and tested (9 new ledger-opening-balances integration tests, 3 existing posting.integration-spec.ts assertions re-verified unchanged, all passing against the real dev database); the frontend has not yet had a real browser pass. Do not mark this slice's UI as done until a browser pass confirms: (1) the legal-entity picker correctly re-fetches the ledger and opening-balance state; (2) Set Opening Balance posts and immediately reflects in the Opening/Closing summary and as the table's first row; (3) Void reverses it and re-enables Set; (4) a second legal entity's view of the same account is unaffected by the first's opening balance; (5) the button is hidden for a role without ledger_opening_balance.set; (6) responsive layout; (7) keyboard/focus behavior; (8) zero browser-console errors.
Phase: Phase 5 -- Finance income vertical slice (Slice 5.4 of an undetermined total -- the fourth slice, picked next per the user's own instruction over Bank Transaction Reconciliation, which this slice was the named prerequisite for).
Task: Ledger Account Opening Balances -- the Ledger Viewer has shown a hard-coded disclaimer since Slice 4.1/4.4 ("no opening balance has been entered for this account, so they do not yet represent this account's real-world balance"); every balance shown has always started from a flat zero since PostingService.getLedgerActivity only ever sums this system's own JournalLine rows, with no mechanism anywhere to record an account's pre-existing (pre-cutover) balance. docs/11's own Phase 5 deliverable list names this directly. This is the least-specified slice built this session -- zero HTML prototype, zero docs/04 screen contract, zero docs/06 posting rule, zero domain-model entity. Three parallel Explore research passes (backend balance-computation code, every doc that could plausibly mention this feature, the existing Chart of Accounts/Journal Entries frontend) surfaced a real, previously-undiscovered tension: docs/11's own phrasing calls for an opening balance "per legal entity/ledger account," but LedgerAccount/AccountGroup are organization-wide, not legal-entity-scoped (ADR-062), and getLedgerActivity had no legal-entity filter at all -- silently summing every legal entity's activity into one shared number. Confirmed with the user via AskUserQuestion before implementation: scope opening balances per legal entity (matching docs/11's literal wording and real accounting practice), which necessarily meant also adding a legal-entity filter to getLedgerActivity itself as part of this slice rather than deferring it. Full rationale in docs/12 ADR-071.
Schema: New LedgerOpeningBalance (legalEntityId/ledgerAccountId/asOfDate/amount/status/journalEntryId/void fields) -- no version/update path at all, immutable once posted (mirrors EMD/SD's lodge-then-return shape, not Invoice's draft/edit shape). Hand-added partial unique index scoping "at most one ACTIVE opening balance" to (organizationId, legalEntityId, ledgerAccountId).
Backend: LedgerOpeningBalancesService.set/void/getForAccount, injecting PrismaService/PostingService only (no ProjectsService -- no project-scoping concern here). set() is not a second balance-computation path: it posts a normal Dr/Cr journal entry (via the existing, unmodified PostingService.postJournal, dated the chosen as-of date) against a new OPENING_BALANCE_EQUITY ledger account (seeded under the pre-existing EQUITY root group, no new group needed) -- getLedgerActivity's existing sum-of-journal-lines formula picks it up automatically with zero change to the balance formula itself. Signed amount convention mirrors getLedgerActivity's own isDebitNormal ? (+debit-credit) : (+credit-debit) formula in reverse: the account itself is debited when a debit-normal account has a non-negative amount, or a credit-normal account has a negative (abnormal-side) amount; equity always takes the opposite side. Rejects a zero amount and a second set while one is already ACTIVE for the same legal-entity+account. void() reuses the existing PostingService.reverseJournal (its fifth reuse this session) against the note's own journalEntryId. PostingService.getLedgerActivity gains one new optional legalEntityId param (backward-compatible -- undefined keeps the exact original all-entities-summed behavior), applied as a journalEntry filter on both its prior-lines query and its in-range query -- the only change made to a pre-existing, already-shipped method this slice. One new permission (ledger_opening_balance.set, Accountant+Admin, reused for both set and void); no new .read permission, since visibility already rides on the existing journal.read. Permission catalog count corrected 73 -> 74.
Frontend: no new route, no new nav item -- this is a nested action within viewing one account's ledger, not a standalone screen. The existing LedgerActivityDrawer (Chart of Accounts page) gains a required "Legal entity" Select (defaulting to the first one) feeding the new legalEntityId param into both journal.getLedgerActivity and a new ledgerOpeningBalances.getForAccount query. Where the stale disclaimer paragraph used to sit: either a short note plus a "Set Opening Balance..." button (a Modal with an as-of-date input and a MoneyInput amount, gated ledger_opening_balance.set), or, once one exists, the balance shown plainly ("Opening balance: X Dr as of DATE") with a "Void..." action (a reason+accounting-date Modal identical in shape to the Journal Entries page's own ReverseJournalModal). No change to the activity table itself.
Files changed: packages/database/prisma/schema.prisma, packages/database/prisma/migrations/20260819120000_phase5_04_ledger_opening_balance (new), packages/database/prisma/seed.ts (OPENING_BALANCE_EQUITY ledger account + one permission + role grant), packages/contracts/src/{ports/ledger-opening-balance.port.ts, routers/ledger-opening-balances.ts} (new), packages/contracts/src/{ports/journal.port.ts, routers/journal.ts} (optional legalEntityId param), wired into context.ts/router.ts/ports/index.ts/index.ts/app.module.ts/main.ts, apps/api/src/accounting/posting.service.ts (getLedgerActivity legal-entity filter), apps/api/src/accounting/posting.integration-spec.ts (three call sites updated), apps/api/src/ledger-opening-balances/{ledger-opening-balances.service.ts, .module.ts, .integration-spec.ts} (all new), apps/api/src/rbac/roles.integration-spec.ts (catalog count 73 -> 74), apps/web/src/app/(platform)/chart-of-accounts/page.tsx (LedgerActivityDrawer updated), docs/11 (5.4 sub-phase entry), docs/12 (ADR-071).
Migrations: phase5_04_ledger_opening_balance -- hand-written, applied via prisma migrate deploy + prisma generate against local Postgres. Purely additive: one new enum, one new table, no backfill.
Tests: 9 new tests in ledger-opening-balances.integration-spec.ts (set posts Dr account/Cr equity for a debit-normal account and the mirrored Cr account/Dr equity for a credit-normal account; a negative abnormal-side amount posts the flipped direction; a second set is rejected while one is already ACTIVE; void reverses the journal and then permits a fresh set; getLedgerActivity's new legalEntityId filter proven to keep two legal entities' opening balances on the very same ledger-account-code fully isolated from each other in both the running balance and the dateFrom-driven prior-lines sum, while omitting the filter reproduces the original summed-together behavior exactly; a zero amount is rejected; idempotent-repeat-returns-original; cross-org isolation) plus 3 existing posting.integration-spec.ts assertions re-verified unchanged after the signature change. Full apps/api integration suite re-run clean (25 files, 270 tests) after the reseed. @zitaware/contracts rebuilds cleanly; apps/api lint/typecheck clean; apps/web lint clean and production build succeeds (no new routes).
Decisions added/changed: ADR-071 (the journal-entry-not-a-second-balance-path mechanism; the OPENING_BALANCE_EQUITY account; the signed-amount convention; the per-legal-entity scoping decision and the resulting getLedgerActivity extension; the at-most-one-ACTIVE-per-legal-entity-and-account rule; the single ledger_opening_balance.set permission covering both set and void; no paginated list -- getForAccount only).
Known issues: apps/web's standalone `npm run typecheck` still fails on the same pre-existing, unrelated, untracked file pair noted in the prior slice (src/proxy.ts/proxy.test.ts) -- unaffected by and unrelated to this slice; Next's own build-time type-check passes clean. No live browser pass was performed this session (no browser-automation tool available) -- see Status above for the specific checklist.
Next task: the outstanding live-browser-verification backlog now spans 5.1, 5.2, the 5.1/5.2 fix pass, the header-bell slice, the Credit/Debit Note slice, and this Ledger Opening Balances slice -- still the natural next checkpoint before further net-new feature work. After that, Bank Transaction Import/Reconciliation is the last remaining Phase 5 deliverable item, now genuinely unblocked; a richer Ledger Viewer redesign (Account/Journal view toggle, Export, expandable entry detail, raised during Slice 4.5 verification) is also newly viable now that opening balances exist.
```

## Invoice Inward Copy -- upload plus list indicator

```text
Date: 2026-08-19
Status: Implementation complete, UI verification pending -- backend fully built and tested (2 new integration tests, all passing against the real dev database); no live browser pass yet. Do not mark this item's UI as done until a browser pass confirms: (1) the "Inward Copy" card on a finalized invoice's detail page uploads and lists correctly, separate from the generic "Documents" card below it; (2) the card is absent while the invoice is still a draft; (3) the global /invoices list's new "Inward Copy" column shows "Attached" once uploaded and "--" beforehand; (4) the project-scoped Invoices tab shows the same; (5) zero browser-console errors.
Phase: Phase 5 polish -- user request, referencing project-invoice-add.html/project-invoices.html directly. docs/04 SS11.4/SS11.5 had already disclosed this exact boundary ("primary tax invoice PDF is generated by CRM; inward acknowledgment remains uploadable"; "show document status and settlement status separately") -- a pre-scoped requirement, not a new invention.
Task: Add an "Inward Copy" upload slot to the invoice detail page (client's stamped acknowledgment of receiving the invoice, optional, addable any time after finalization) and a matching "Inward Copy" column on both invoice list surfaces. Reuses the existing generic Documents module rather than a new model -- DocumentLink.linkType (a field that already existed but was never populated by any caller) now carries 'INWARD_COPY' for this specific tag; "has an inward copy" is a live EXISTS query, never stored, matching the outstandingAmount/opening-balance precedent.
Backend: documents.listLinksForEntity gains an optional linkType filter (undefined = unchanged existing behavior). InvoicesService.list()/getDetail() gain hasInwardCopy: boolean via a batched documentLink lookup (list) or a single findFirst (getDetail), mirroring the existing settledByInvoiceId/netCreditDebitByInvoiceId batching pattern. No new permission -- reuses document.upload/manage/download. No schema migration -- linkType already existed on DocumentLink.
Frontend: components/documents-tab.tsx (shared across Lead/Tender/Project/Invoice/Receipt/Security-Instrument) gains optional linkType/uploadCardTitle/listCardTitle/emptyText props, all defaulting to today's exact generic behavior. The invoice detail page gains a second, tagged DocumentsTab instance ("Inward Copy", linkType='INWARD_COPY') alongside the existing untagged one ("Documents", for free-form attachments), visible only once the invoice is FINALIZED or VOID (mirrors the existing Credit/Debit Notes card gating). Both /invoices and the project Invoices tab gain an "Inward Copy" column rendering plain colored text ("Attached"/"--"), deliberately not a StatusTag pill, matching project-invoices.html's own visual contract exactly.
Files changed: packages/contracts/src/{ports/invoice.port.ts, ports/document.port.ts, routers/documents.ts, ports/index.ts, index.ts}, apps/api/src/documents/documents.service.ts, apps/api/src/invoices/invoices.service.ts, apps/web/src/components/documents-tab.tsx, apps/web/src/app/(platform)/invoices/shared.tsx, apps/web/src/app/(platform)/invoices/page.tsx, apps/web/src/app/(platform)/projects/[id]/invoice-tab.tsx, apps/web/src/app/(platform)/projects/[id]/invoices/[invoiceId]/page.tsx.
Migrations: none -- DocumentLink.linkType already existed.
Tests: 1 new test in documents.integration-spec.ts (linkType filter isolates a tagged link from an untagged/differently-tagged one on the same entity; omitting the filter still returns everything) and 1 new test in invoices.integration-spec.ts (hasInwardCopy false on a fresh finalized invoice in both getDetail/list; a SUPPORTING-tagged link doesn't flip it; an INWARD_COPY-tagged link does, in both). Full apps/api integration suite re-run clean (25 files, 272 tests). @zitaware/contracts rebuilds cleanly; apps/api/apps/web lint and typecheck clean; apps/web production build succeeds with no new routes.
Decisions added/changed: ADR-072 (linkType-based document tagging instead of a new model/column; DocumentsTab extended with optional props instead of a bespoke widget; card gated to non-draft invoices; plain-text list indicator instead of a pill; applied to both list surfaces).
Known issues: two real bugs shipped and were fixed within this same change, both caught live by the user, neither caught by typecheck/lint/build. (1) The invoice detail page imported the new INVOICE_INWARD_COPY_LINK_TYPE constant as a runtime value from @zitaware/contracts, whose index.ts barrel also re-exports the server-only tRPC init; Next bundled the whole package into the browser and crashed with "You're trying to use @trpc/server in a non-server environment." Fixed by duplicating the literal locally in apps/web/src/app/(platform)/invoices/shared.tsx instead of importing it -- matches the pre-existing, now-reinforced convention that every apps/web import from @zitaware/contracts must be `import type`, never a value. (2) The generic "Documents" DocumentsTab instance queried listLinksForEntity with linkType: undefined, which meant "no filter" -- so an invoice's INWARD_COPY-tagged document also showed up in the plain Documents list below it, duplicated across both cards. Fixed by widening listLinksForEntity's linkType to a real three-state contract (undefined = everything, unchanged; null = untagged only; a string = that exact tag only) and having DocumentsTab's list query/invalidation pass `linkType ?? null` instead of the raw prop, so a generic (no-linkType) instance now only ever shows untagged links. The upload/link mutation itself was already correct and unchanged. Otherwise none, besides the outstanding live-browser-verification backlog noted in Status above.
Next task: Bank Transaction Import/Reconciliation remains the one open Phase 5 deliverable, or the richer Ledger Viewer redesign -- whichever the user prioritizes next.
```
