## Phase 6, Slice 6.1: Vendor Master + Purchase Request

```text
Date: 2026-08-20
Status: Implementation complete, UI verification pending -- backend fully built and tested (7 new vendors integration tests, 23 new purchase-requests integration tests, all passing against the real dev database); the frontend has not yet had a real browser pass. Do not mark this slice's UI as done until a browser pass confirms: (1) adding a vendor from scratch and via "use existing party," and seeing both the hard GSTIN-duplicate error and the soft name/city warning; (2) raising a purchase request end-to-end for all three GST paths (Yes-intrastate, Yes-interstate, No) with the live preview matching what actually saves; (3) save-draft -> upload document -> submit flow; (4) an Accountant/PM seeing but not self-approving a request, then approving someone else's and rejecting another with a reason; (5) an Admin's self-approve flow (hidden without the permission, requires a note, shows as self-approved in history); (6) Employee has no approve action anywhere and CEO is read-only; (7) cancel from draft and from submitted; (8) list filters/columns/status pills with no payment-related fields anywhere; (9) responsive/keyboard/zero-console-error pass.
Phase: Phase 6 -- Procurement and expense (Slice 6.1, the first slice -- chosen over Direct Expense/Claim/Advance as the most bounded starting point, since Purchase Request submission has zero accounting effect).
Task: Vendor Master + Purchase Request (draft/submit/approve/reject/cancel) -- the user described the vendor-purchase flow with HTML references (purchase-request.html/purchase-list.html/master-vendor.html) and asked which Phase 6 vertical to build first; confirmed via AskUserQuestion. An initial implementation plan was rejected twice with detailed, itemized feedback -- vendor duplicate handling, request numbering, removing duplicate amount entry, immutable approval history, maker-checker role-matrix correction, idempotency, referential integrity, document-upload sequencing, frontend nav simplification, and (a third, narrower round) replacing an absolute self-approval prohibition with a permission-controlled exception -- before being approved. Full rationale in docs/12 ADR-074.
Schema: New VendorProfile (a second role layered on the existing Party foundation, exactly as ClientProfile's own doc comment anticipated) with normalized/format-validated GSTIN and PAN, a partial-unique GSTIN index, and a controlled VendorPaymentTerms enum. New PurchaseRequest/PurchaseRequestLine/PurchaseRequestDecision -- zero accounting effect at any status; requestNumber (PR-000001) assigned at draft creation; lines are the sole source of the taxable/estimated base amount; request-level GST/ITC/supply-type fields are a requester-entered estimate enforced consistent by a hand-written CHECK constraint; PurchaseRequestDecision is an append-only maker-checker ledger with an isSelfApproval flag.
Backend: VendorsService (list/getDetail/checkForDuplicates/create/update/setActive) mirrors ClientsService's own shape. Found and fixed a real latent bug this slice's multi-role Party design exposed: ClientsService.setActive/list previously coupled Party.isActive to the Client role's own flag alone -- now each role's setActive touches only its own profile, and Party.isActive is a true independent party-level switch. PurchaseRequestsService (createDraft/updateDraft/submit/approve/reject/cancel/list/getDetail/calculatePreview) reuses the existing project.read_all-driven assigned-project scoping mechanism verbatim (no new scoping dimension), the existing generateInvoiceNumber-style concurrency-safe numbering pattern, and the existing IdempotencyKey/hashRequest mechanism on every transition. One pure computeTotals helper is reused by calculatePreview and every real command, so the live UI preview and what actually persists can never disagree. Maker-checker: creator cannot approve their own request unless they hold the new purchase_request.self_approve permission (Admin only, by default) and supply a mandatory justification note, permanently flagged on the decision row. New EMPLOYEE_PERMISSIONS grant of project.read (verified safe: unlocks only project list/detail/team/progress, not any other finance module's own separately-gated data). Six new permissions; permission catalog count corrected 74 -> 80 (reseeded and verified against the live dev DB).
Frontend: A new "Purchases & Expenses" nav group (not two similarly-named "Finance" groups) holding /vendors (an inline master-vendor.html-style add-form + list, not the app's more common Drawer pattern -- a deliberate visual-fidelity choice, including a duplicate-match modal offering "use this existing party") and /purchase-requests (list/new/detail, matching purchase-request.html's three form sections and purchase-list.html's columns minus every Payment-related tile/column, since no payment tracking exists until a future Vendor Bill/Payment slice). The detail page splits into a DraftEditor (Save Draft/Submit/Cancel) and a submitted/decided view (Approve/Reject/Cancel actions, Documents tab via entityType='PURCHASE_REQUEST', an Approval History section showing each decision with an explicit "Self-approved" tag when applicable).
Files changed: packages/database/prisma/schema.prisma, packages/database/prisma/migrations/20260820090000_phase6_01_vendor_purchase_request (new, hand-curated from a prisma migrate diff run), packages/database/prisma/seed.ts (six permissions + role grants + one EntityType row), apps/api/src/clients/clients.service.ts (setActive/list fix), apps/api/src/vendors/{vendors.service.ts, .module.ts, .integration-spec.ts} (new), apps/api/src/purchase-requests/{purchase-requests.service.ts, .module.ts, .integration-spec.ts} (new), apps/api/src/app.module.ts, apps/api/src/main.ts, apps/api/src/rbac/roles.integration-spec.ts (catalog count 74 -> 80), packages/contracts/src/{ports/vendor.port.ts, ports/purchase-request.port.ts, routers/vendors.ts, routers/purchase-requests.ts} (new), wired into context.ts/router.ts/ports/index.ts/index.ts, apps/web/src/app/(platform)/vendors/page.tsx (new), apps/web/src/app/(platform)/purchase-requests/{page.tsx, new/page.tsx, [id]/page.tsx, shared.tsx} (new), apps/web/src/components/documents-tab.tsx (PURCHASE_REQUEST entityType), packages/ui/src/components/{icons.tsx, index.ts, nav-items.ts} (two new icons, one new nav group), docs/11 (6.1 sub-phase entry), docs/12 (ADR-074).
Migrations: phase6_01_vendor_purchase_request -- hand-curated from `prisma migrate diff` (three unrelated pre-existing hand-added constraints/indexes the raw diff also proposed dropping, since Prisma's schema DSL can't represent them, were deliberately excluded), applied via prisma migrate deploy + prisma generate against local Postgres. Purely additive: five new enums, four new tables, no backfill.
Tests: 7 new tests in vendors.integration-spec.ts (new-Party and existing-Party-attach creation; independent GSTIN/PAN format rejection; exact-GSTIN-duplicate rejection; Client-GSTIN cross-match found and resolved via existingPartyId; soft NAME_CITY match never blocking; the dual-role Party independence regression; list scoping/cross-org isolation) and 23 new tests in purchase-requests.integration-spec.ts (sequential immutable request numbering; empty-lines rejection; CANCELLED-project-blocks/ON_HOLD-allows; inactive-vendor/inactive-category rejection; GST-No derives estimatedAmount and clears every GST field; GST-Yes intrastate/interstate compute and clear the opposite tax component; missing-supplyType rejection; calculatePreview matches submit's own persisted numbers exactly; idempotent create; updateDraft rejected post-submit; partial updateDraft preserves untouched fields; self-approval rejected without the permission and without a note, succeeds with both, flags isSelfApproval=true; a non-creator approves normally; reject requires a reason; REJECTED is terminal; idempotent approve creates no duplicate decision row; PM-equivalent scoped approve succeeds only for an assigned project; cancel is creator-only and works from DRAFT/SUBMITTED; stale-version rejection; scoped list/getDetail; cross-org isolation). Full apps/api integration suite re-run clean (27 files, 302 tests). @zitaware/contracts/@zitaware/ui rebuild cleanly; apps/api/apps/web lint and typecheck clean; apps/web production build succeeds with all four new routes registered.
Decisions added/changed: ADR-074 (VendorProfile as a second Party role; the ClientsService setActive/list independence fix; requestNumber assigned at draft creation; lines as the sole amount source; the GST-fields-consistency enforcement and its CHECK constraint; the append-only PurchaseRequestDecision ledger; the corrected maker-checker role matrix with the self-approve exception; the terminal REJECTED/CANCELLED lifecycle; the document-upload sequencing; the project operational-status gating default; the single "Purchases & Expenses" nav group).
Known issues: none besides the outstanding live-browser-verification backlog noted in Status above.
Next task: Bank Transaction Import/Reconciliation remains deferred (ADR-073). Within Phase 6, the natural next slice is Direct Expense (the simplest remaining vertical, no Vendor dependency, posts immediately) or continuing the procurement chain toward Vendor Bill/Payment (where GST/TDS postings actually become real) -- whichever the user prioritizes next.
```

## Phase 6, Slice 6.2: Vendor Bill

```text
Date: 2026-08-20
Status: Implementation complete, UI verification pending -- backend fully built and tested (16 new vendor-bills integration tests, all passing against the real dev database); the frontend has not yet had a real browser pass. Do not mark this slice's UI as done until a browser pass confirms: (1) raising a vendor bill from an approved purchase request's own page, both intrastate and interstate, with mixed ITC-eligible/ineligible lines; (2) the Save as Draft / Save & Finalize two-button flow behaves correctly, including the loading/error states; (3) the resulting journal (via Journal Entries / Chart-of-Accounts Ledger Viewer) matches the expected Dr Expense (grouped by category) + Dr Input GST Receivable (eligible only) / Cr Accounts Payable -- Vendor split; (4) void of both a draft and a finalized bill, including the reversal; (5) the Vendor Bills card on the Purchase Request detail page lists bills correctly and the "Add Vendor Bill" action only appears once APPROVED and only for a permitted user; (6) PM sees only their assigned projects' bills, Accountant/Admin see everything; (7) responsive/keyboard/zero-console-error pass.
Phase: Phase 6 -- Procurement and expense (Slice 6.2, the second slice -- the first real accounting entry on the purchase side, chosen by the user via AskUserQuestion as the natural follow-on to 6.1).
Task: Vendor Bill (draft/finalize/void, GST + Input GST Receivable + Accounts Payable posting) -- user asked to move to the next slice; AskUserQuestion offered Vendor Bill as the recommended option and it was selected, followed by three design-fork questions (per-line vs whole-bill ITC eligibility; capped vs uncapped against the Purchase Request estimate; single vs multiple bills per request), all resolved by picking the recommended option. The resulting plan was approved with zero revisions -- a first for this session's Phase 6 work. Full rationale in docs/12 ADR-075.
Schema: ExpenseCategory gained a nullable ledgerAccountId (mirrors PenaltyCategory's own nullable-with-fallback shape), closing a gap surfaced while separately answering the user's own Chart-of-Accounts question. New VendorBill/VendorBillLine -- structurally InvoiceLine's own twin plus expenseCategoryId (resolves the debit account) and a per-line itcEligible boolean. purchaseRequestId is not unique (multiple bills per request, confirmed decision). A hand-added partial-unique index on (organization_id, vendor_id, vendor_bill_number) WHERE status != 'VOID' prevents recording the same vendor invoice twice while letting a voided entry's number be reused. No hand-added CHECK constraint on line amounts, matching InvoiceLine's own precedent -- validation lives in the shared computeLines() helper.
Backend: VendorBillsService (getDraftDefaults/createDraft/updateDraft/finalize/void/list/getDetail) reuses computeLines (its third caller, after CreditDebitNotesService) in createDraft/updateDraft only; finalize reads already-frozen line amounts, matching Invoice.finalize's own real behavior rather than recomputing GST. finalize groups/sums cost debits by each line's resolved ledger account (expenseCategoryId -> ExpenseCategory.ledgerAccountId, falling back to a seeded OTHER_EXPENSES account) so several lines under one category collapse into a single journal debit, sums eligible CGST/SGST/IGST separately into up to three Input GST Receivable debit lines, and credits one Accounts Payable -- Vendor line for the bill's full total (taxable + all GST, eligible or not -- it's still owed to the vendor). void mirrors Invoice.void exactly. Posted via the existing, unmodified PostingService.postJournal/reverseJournal. Five new permissions; PM scoping reuses the existing ProjectsService-backed mechanism, applied to every mutating method (not just list/getDetail) as a stricter posture than Invoice's own write paths. Permission catalog count corrected 80 -> 85 (reseeded and verified against the live dev DB).
Frontend: /vendor-bills (list/new/detail) as full pages, not drawers -- per the user's own earlier "drawer is not comfortable to CEO" feedback from this same session, adopting the Save as Draft / Save & Finalize two-button pattern from the start rather than needing a second round of feedback to get there. A new Vendor Bills card on the Purchase Request detail page appears once status is APPROVED, listing bills raised against it (with the request's own estimate shown for reference only) plus an "Add Vendor Bill" action. New VendorBillIcon (inbound document/tray glyph) in a new "Vendor Bills" nav item under the existing "Purchases & Expenses" group.
Files changed: packages/database/prisma/schema.prisma, packages/database/prisma/migrations/20260821090000_phase6_02_vendor_bill (new, hand-curated from a prisma migrate diff run), packages/database/prisma/seed.ts (new Accounts Payable + Input GST Receivable x3 + Vendor Expenses ledger accounts, ExpenseCategory.ledgerAccountId backfill, five permissions + role grants + one EntityType row), apps/api/src/vendor-bills/{vendor-bills.service.ts, .module.ts, .integration-spec.ts} (new), apps/api/src/app.module.ts, apps/api/src/main.ts, apps/api/src/rbac/roles.integration-spec.ts (catalog count 80 -> 85), packages/contracts/src/{ports/vendor-bill.port.ts, routers/vendor-bills.ts} (new), wired into context.ts/router.ts/ports/index.ts/index.ts, apps/web/src/app/(platform)/vendor-bills/{page.tsx, new/page.tsx, [id]/page.tsx, shared.tsx} (new), apps/web/src/app/(platform)/purchase-requests/[id]/page.tsx (Vendor Bills card), apps/web/src/components/documents-tab.tsx (VENDOR_BILL entityType), packages/ui/src/components/{icons.tsx, nav-items.ts} (one new icon, one new nav item), docs/11 (6.2 sub-phase entry), docs/12 (ADR-075).
Migrations: phase6_02_vendor_bill -- hand-curated from `prisma migrate diff` (the same three unrelated pre-existing hand-added constraints/indexes the raw diff always proposes dropping were excluded again), applied via prisma migrate deploy + prisma generate against local Postgres. Purely additive: one new enum, two new tables, one new nullable column on expense_categories, no backfill required (existing rows simply get a null ledgerAccountId until the seed's own backfill runs).
Tests: 16 new tests in vendor-bills.integration-spec.ts (blocked until the source Purchase Request is APPROVED, on both getDraftDefaults and createDraft; vendor/project/legal-entity pre-filled from the request and uncapped against its estimate; duplicate vendorBillNumber per vendor rejected while active, reusable once voided; multiple bills against one request all succeed; inactive expense category rejected; idempotent createDraft; intra-state CGST+SGST computed and ITC-eligible vs ineligible correctly splits Input GST Receivable vs folded-into-cost, with same-category lines collapsing into one grouped journal debit; inter-state IGST; fallback to OTHER_EXPENSES for an unmapped category; idempotent finalize posts no second journal; finalize rejected on an already-finalized bill; void of a finalized bill reverses the journal; void requires a reason and, for a finalized bill, an accounting date; PM project-scoping on create; stale-expectedVersion rejection; cross-org isolation). Full apps/api integration suite shows contention-driven timeouts only under the complete 29-file parallel run (a pre-existing dev-Postgres connection-pool limitation -- confirmed by re-running the exact four "failed" files, including this slice's own, together in isolation: all 50 tests pass cleanly); not a functional regression. @zitaware/contracts/@zitaware/ui rebuild cleanly; apps/api/apps/web lint and typecheck clean; apps/web production build succeeds with all three new routes registered.
Decisions added/changed: ADR-075 (ExpenseCategory.ledgerAccountId; the VendorBill/VendorBillLine shape; per-line ITC eligibility; uncapped against the Purchase Request estimate; multiple bills per request; finalize reading frozen line amounts and grouping cost debits by resolved ledger account; void mirroring Invoice.void; the five new permissions and stricter PM-scoping posture; the full-page not-drawer UI direction).
Known issues: none besides the outstanding live-browser-verification backlog noted in Status above, and the pre-existing full-suite parallel-run test contention noted in Tests above (an environment/test-harness characteristic, not scoped to this slice to fix).
Next task: Bank Transaction Import/Reconciliation remains deferred (ADR-073). Within Phase 6, the natural next slice continues the procurement chain toward Payment Request/Vendor Payment (where TDS posting becomes real, per docs/04's purchase-payment.html contract) or Direct Expense (the simplest remaining vertical, no Vendor dependency) -- whichever the user prioritizes next.
```

## Phase 6, Slice 6.2 polish: units of measure, live line-summary panel, GST rate/amount entry, budget-variance tracking

```text
Date: 2026-08-21
Status: Implementation complete, UI verification pending -- backend fully built and tested (6 new vendor-bills integration tests plus a corrected masters catalog-count assertion, all passing against the real dev database); the frontend has not yet had a real browser pass. Do not mark this polish pass as done until a browser pass confirms: (1) the Live Summary panel updates as quantity/unit price/GST are entered on both the New and Draft-Edit pages; (2) typing a GST rate fills the amount and vice versa, staying in sync as quantity/price change; (3) the Unit dropdown defaults to Qty and is editable via Masters > Units of Measure; (4) finalizing a bill that pushes cumulative billing past the request's estimate shows the warning but does not block; (5) the color highlight appears correctly on the Vendor Bill list, detail page, and the Purchase Request's own Vendor Bills card.
Phase: Phase 6 -- Procurement and expense (a polish pass on Slice 6.2, not a new numbered slice -- same framing as the Invoice Inward Copy follow-up in Phase 5).
Task: The user reviewed the freshly-shipped Add Vendor Bill page live in the running app and asked for four things: a live per-line summary (modeled on the Client Receipt "Record Payment" page), GST enterable as a rate or a raw amount (also modeled on that page), a unit-of-measure dropdown on quantity, and some way to see when billing against a Purchase Request runs past its own estimate (a non-blocking confirmation plus a color highlight -- the uncapped design itself, from ADR-075, stays unchanged). AskUserQuestion resolved the units-of-measure design (admin-manageable master with a non-editable code) before planning; the plan was then approved with zero revisions. Full rationale in docs/12 ADR-076.
Schema: New UnitOfMeasure model -- a 13th generic MasterTypeKey, not a bespoke model, reusing the existing Masters system's list/create/update/setActive/getCatalog wholesale (confirmed zero per-type special-casing exists in masters.service.ts). VendorBillLine.unitOfMeasureId is nullable at the DB layer only -- while checking the table before migrating, one real, already-VOID row was found (the user had already tried the live feature), with no UnitOfMeasure data to backfill it from; every new create/update call still requires it via a required zod field, same nullable-at-DB/required-in-practice shape ExpenseCategory.ledgerAccountId already established.
Backend: New vendorBills.calculatePreview -- pure, non-mutating, reuses the same computeLines() call createDraft/updateDraft persist with, so the live preview can never disagree with what actually saves (mirrors receipts.calculatePreview's own "one calculation, reused by preview and the real command" shape). VendorBillSummary (and therefore VendorBillDetail) gained live purchaseRequestEstimatedAmount/cumulativeBilledAmount fields -- the latter a batched groupBy in list() (mirroring InvoicesService.list()'s own outstandingAmount pattern) or a single aggregate elsewhere, summing every FINALIZED non-void bill against the same purchase request; a bill still in DRAFT naturally excludes itself, a FINALIZED one naturally includes itself from then on. VendorBillDraftDefaults gained the same cumulativeBilledAmount so the New Vendor Bill page's own warning accounts for prior bills too. Caught and fixed a real gap during typecheck: packages/contracts/src/routers/masters.ts has its own independent hardcoded zod enum for master types, separate from the MasterTypeKey port type -- both needed the new 'units_of_measure' value.
Frontend: The New Vendor Bill page and the Draft Editor on the detail page are both reworked into the Record Payment page's own two-column sticky layout, with the identical debounced-calculatePreview-call pattern powering a per-line "Live Summary" panel. Each line's GST rate/amount fields cross-fill each other client-side (a data-entry convenience, not a new source of truth -- only gstRatePercent is ever submitted, the DB still only stores computed amounts) via the same mechanic the Record Payment page's own deduction Rate%/Amount pair already uses, requiring the line editor to be restructured into a per-row component since each row needs its own Form.useWatch (disallowed inside a bare .map() by react-hooks/rules-of-hooks). A shared varianceTone() helper colors the amount red when cumulative billing exceeds the request's estimate, reused on the Vendor Bill list, the finalized/void detail view's new "Cumulative billed vs. estimate" line, and the Purchase Request's own Vendor Bills card.
Files changed: packages/database/prisma/schema.prisma, packages/database/prisma/migrations/20260821100000_phase6_02b_vendor_bill_units_and_variance (new), packages/database/prisma/seed.ts (UNITS_OF_MEASURE + legacy-row backfill), packages/contracts/src/ports/master.port.ts, packages/contracts/src/ports/vendor-bill.port.ts, packages/contracts/src/routers/masters.ts, packages/contracts/src/routers/vendor-bills.ts, packages/contracts/src/{ports/index.ts, index.ts}, apps/api/src/masters/masters.service.ts, apps/api/src/vendor-bills/vendor-bills.service.ts, apps/api/src/vendor-bills/vendor-bills.integration-spec.ts, apps/api/src/masters/masters.integration-spec.ts (catalog count 13 -> 14), apps/web/src/app/(platform)/masters/catalog.ts, apps/web/src/app/(platform)/vendor-bills/{shared.tsx, new/page.tsx, [id]/page.tsx, page.tsx}, apps/web/src/app/(platform)/purchase-requests/[id]/page.tsx.
Migrations: phase6_02b_vendor_bill_units_and_variance -- hand-curated from `prisma migrate diff` (the same three unrelated pre-existing constraints/indexes always excluded), applied via prisma migrate deploy + prisma generate. Purely additive: one new table, one new nullable column, no other backfill required beyond the seed script's own one-time legacy-row fix.
Tests: 6 new tests in vendor-bills.integration-spec.ts (calculatePreview matches what createDraft/finalize actually persist, intrastate and interstate; empty-lines and non-approved-request handling; inactive unit rejected same as inactive category; cumulativeBilledAmount/purchaseRequestEstimatedAmount correct across two bills against one request, before and after finalize, reported consistently by list()/getDetail(), and dropping a voided bill back out) -- 20/20 vendor-bills tests passing. masters.integration-spec.ts's catalog-count assertion corrected 13 -> 14, 6/6 masters tests passing. @zitaware/contracts rebuilds cleanly; apps/api/apps/web lint, typecheck, and production build all clean.
Decisions added/changed: ADR-076 (units of measure as a 13th generic master with a nullable-for-one-legacy-row FK; calculatePreview reusing computeLines; the GST rate/amount two-way frontend convenience; the shared purchaseRequestEstimatedAmount/cumulativeBilledAmount fields powering both the soft-friction finalize warning and the list/detail/card color variance).
Known issues: none besides the outstanding live-browser-verification backlog noted in Status above.
Next task: Bank Transaction Import/Reconciliation remains deferred (ADR-073). Within Phase 6, the natural next slice continues toward Payment Request/Vendor Payment or Direct Expense -- whichever the user prioritizes next.

## Cross-cutting UI: Drawer -> Page conversion, Batch 1

```text
Date: 2026-08-21
Status: Implementation complete, UI verification pending -- backend fully built and tested (3 new audit.getById integration tests, all passing), typecheck/lint/build clean across all 9 workspaces, full apps/api integration suite passing 28/28 files (328/328 tests) after a re-run that ruled out a first-pass connection-pool-contention false failure; the frontend has not yet had a real browser pass. Vendors -- the Edit Drawer becomes a second inline "Editing {vendor}" card, mirroring the existing inline "Add Vendor" card exactly, no new route. Journal Entries -- EntryDetailDrawer becomes /journal-entries/[id]; its in-drawer original/reversal cross-navigation becomes real router.push page links, a genuine UX improvement. Audit Log -- AuditDetailDrawer becomes /audit-log/[id], backed by the new getById endpoint. Chart of Accounts -- LedgerActivityDrawer becomes /chart-of-accounts/ledger/[accountId]; its two nested opening-balance Set/Void Modals are carried over unchanged, explicitly out of scope this round. The Chart of Accounts Group/Account create/edit Drawers are deliberately untouched this batch (Batch 2 scope -- no existing detail route to land on yet).

Clients (originally scoped to Batch 2) shipped early, on direct user request -- see the entry below.

## Cross-cutting UI: Drawer -> Page layout fixes, then Clients module conversion

```text
Date: 2026-08-21
Status: Implementation complete, UI verification pending -- typecheck/lint/build clean across all 9 workspaces on every pass; the frontend has not yet had a real browser pass. Do not mark this work's UI as done until a browser pass confirms: (1) Leads/Tenders/Projects/Purchase Requests Add and Edit pages no longer show a wide header over a narrow, awkwardly-gapped card, and fields render 3-per-row on desktop, 2-per-row on tablet, 1-per-row on mobile; (2) the new /clients/new and /clients/[id] pages work end to end -- add, view, inline edit, contacts repeatable rows, state/district/city cascade; (3) the Leads/Projects lists on a client's own detail page link correctly to their own detail pages, and the "Total Project Value" tile sums correctly.
Phase: Cross-cutting UI (not phase-numbered) -- two follow-on requests against the Batch 1 Drawer-to-page conversion (ADR-077): a layout-quality bug report, then an explicit request to bring the Clients module (originally scoped to Batch 2) forward.
Task: After Batch 1 shipped, the user reported the newly-converted Add/Edit pages showed fields stretched full width with no sensible grouping, and separately that the page container looked short with unnecessary white space on the right once fields were grouped. Root-caused to two compounding issues: (1) `AppShell`'s shared `<main>` is capped at 1500px (correct for wide list/dashboard pages) while only the inner `UiCard` had been narrowed for these forms, leaving `PageHeader` full-width above a visibly narrower card; (2) field groups used inconsistent column counts with no tablet-specific breakpoint. Fixed by wrapping `PageHeader` + the form card together in one `max-w-[1040px]` container (matching both docs/15's own "Standard form" width spec and the literal `max-width:1040px` hardcoded in the paired HTML references, `lead-add.html`/`project-add.html`), and standardizing every short-field group on `grid-cols-1 sm:grid-cols-2 lg:grid-cols-3`. Applied to Leads/Tenders/Projects (new + inline edit) and, since Purchase Requests' Add/Draft-edit pages had the identical defect (built earlier this session, before the pattern was corrected), to those too. Vendor Bills' new/edit pages were left untouched -- their two-column live-calculation layout is a deliberately different, already-correct "wide finance form" pattern (ADR-076), not the same defect class. Once the pattern was solid, the user asked directly for the Clients module (until now still a Drawer, and the only CRM-family module with no `[id]` detail page at all) to get the same create/edit-page treatment.
Backend: None -- both pieces are pure frontend chrome/layout; the Clients conversion reuses the existing `clients.list`/`getDetail`/`create`/`update`/`setActive` mutations verbatim, and reads `leads.list`/`projects.list` client-side (already org-scoped, unfiltered) to derive a client's own Leads/Projects sections -- the same client-side-filter-from-a-full-list pattern already used throughout this session, so no new backend endpoint was needed.
Frontend: Layout fix applied to `leads/{page,new,[id]}.tsx`, `tenders/{page,new,[id]}.tsx`, `projects/{page,new,[id]}.tsx`, `purchase-requests/{new,[id]}/page.tsx`, and (breakpoint-only, width unchanged by design) `vendors/page.tsx`. Clients conversion: extracted the Drawer-only `ClientFormBody` into a new `clients/shared.tsx` (matching the Lead/Tender/Project/Vendor-Bill `shared.tsx` convention), grouping its short fields into the same 3-tier grid from the start; `clients/page.tsx` lost its Drawer and now routes "Add Client" to `/clients/new` and each row's "View" to `/clients/[id]`; `clients/new/page.tsx` is new; `clients/[id]/page.tsx` is an entirely new detail page (previously did not exist for this module) built against `client-view.html`'s own layout (`MINOR_CHANGE` treatment per docs/16) -- identity header with initials badge, a 4-tile stats row (Leads count, Projects count, Total Project Value summed client-side from that client's own projects, Client Since), a Client Information card, a two-column Leads/Projects list (each row linking to that record's own existing detail page, with the Lead/Project module's own `StageBadge` reused for the status pill), and a Contacts card -- plus the same inline `editing`-toggle pattern as Leads/Tenders/Projects for the Edit flow. The reference HTML's "Receivable" stat tile was deliberately dropped -- it would require a new cross-module invoice/receipt outstanding-amount aggregation, out of scope for a page-chrome conversion; flagged here rather than silently omitted.
Files changed: apps/web/src/app/(platform)/{leads,tenders,projects}/{page.tsx,new/page.tsx,[id]/page.tsx} (width/grid fixes), apps/web/src/app/(platform)/purchase-requests/{new,[id]}/page.tsx (same fixes), apps/web/src/app/(platform)/vendors/page.tsx (breakpoint only), apps/web/src/app/(platform)/clients/shared.tsx (new), apps/web/src/app/(platform)/clients/new/page.tsx (new), apps/web/src/app/(platform)/clients/[id]/page.tsx (new), apps/web/src/app/(platform)/clients/page.tsx (Drawer removed).
Migrations: none -- no schema or backend change.
Tests: none added -- no business logic changed; every mutation/query call is byte-for-byte the same as before. Full `turbo run typecheck lint build` clean across all 9 workspaces on every pass (layout fix pass and Clients-conversion pass verified separately); `apps/web` production build registers `/clients`, `/clients/new`, `/clients/[id]` alongside every route from the Batch 1 pass.
Decisions added/changed: none requiring a new ADR -- both pieces are corrections/extensions of ADR-077's own already-recorded pattern, not a new architectural decision.
Known issues: none besides the outstanding live-browser-verification backlog noted in Status above. The dropped "Receivable" stat tile (noted under Frontend) is a deliberate, disclosed scope boundary, not an oversight.
Next task: Batch 2's remaining modules (generic Masters screen + states/districts/cities/penalty-categories, Legal Entities, Bank Accounts, Financial Years, Chart of Accounts groups/accounts, Roles, Introducers, Employees) and Batch 3 (EMD/SD, Milestones) remain as documented future work, each starting only after a short confirmation, per the approved plan.
```

## Phase 6, Slice 6.3: Payment Request (authorization/reservation workflow, zero accounting effect)

```text
Date: 2026-08-21
Status: Implementation complete, UI verification pending -- backend fully built and tested (26 new tests, all passing, including a genuine concurrent-submission race test), typecheck/lint/build clean across all 9 workspaces (27/27 tasks), a fresh-database migration replay on a disposable scratch database confirmed the full migration history applies cleanly from zero, and the full apps/api integration suite passed 29/29 files (354/354 tests) on a clean first run; the frontend has not yet had a real browser pass. Do not mark this slice's UI as done until a browser pass confirms: (1) the New Payment Request page's Legal Entity auto-select/picker, eligible-bills checklist, and live preview total all behave correctly for both a manual flow and a `?vendorBillId=` seeded flow; (2) the Draft editor's bill re-selection and Submit/Cancel modals behave correctly; (3) Approve/Reject/Revoke/Cancel actions on a submitted/approved/decided request show and hide correctly per permission and self-approval state; (4) the Vendor Bill detail page's new "Payment Requests" card and its "Raise Payment Request..." visibility (only while a balance remains) work correctly.
Phase: Phase 6 -- Procurement and expense (Slice 6.3 of an undetermined total -- the third slice, continuing docs/04's own procurement chain from Purchase Request (6.1) and Vendor Bill (6.2) toward Vendor Payment).
Task: User asked what the next slice was; AskUserQuestion offered Payment Request/Vendor Payment, Direct Expense, and the two remaining Drawer-to-page batches -- Payment Request/Vendor Payment was chosen. Two design forks were confirmed via AskUserQuestion before planning: multiple bills batched into one Payment Request (chosen over one-bill-per-request), and partial payment requests allowed across multiple requests over time (chosen over requiring a bill requested in full). A first plan was rejected outright with an exceptionally detailed 13-point review largely specifying the correct architecture directly -- gross-liability semantics with TDS explicitly excluded; concurrency-safe row-locked outstanding enforcement; a DB-level unique constraint on lines; a new REVOKED status/lifecycle; a precise self-approval/permission matrix; strict "no inaccessible line exists" project-scoping for multi-project batches; a Legal Entity selection workflow; a simplified form dropping title/description for requestedPaymentDate; a server-authoritative non-mutating preview; a shared sequential-number-generator extraction reused by Purchase Request too; rich audit/decision-history requirements; and an explicit rejection of "an immediate rerun passed" as an acceptable resolution for the connection-pool timeout pattern already disclosed in ADR-075/076/077, demanding a real config fix instead. The revised plan addressed every point by section number and was approved with no further changes. Full rationale in docs/12 ADR-078.
Backend: New PaymentRequest/PaymentRequestLine/PaymentRequestDecision models. requestedAmount is the gross vendor liability amount authorized for settlement -- no TDS field, computation, or mention anywhere in this slice. availableForRequest = vendorBill.totalAmount minus sum(requestedAmount) across every line whose parent status is SUBMITTED/APPROVED, computed by one shared getAvailableForRequest helper reused by createDraft/updateDraft (soft), submit (hard, authoritative), calculatePreview, getEligibleBills, and getBillAvailability -- never duplicated. submit is the sole authoritative reservation gate: inside one transaction it locks every referenced vendor bill row (SELECT ... FOR UPDATE, ascending id order -- deadlock-safe against overlapping concurrent submits) and revalidates live availability before flipping DRAFT to SUBMITTED; any line that no longer fits rolls back the entire submission. A new REVOKED status lets an APPROVED request be withdrawn (new payment_request.revoke permission, mandatory reason), releasing its reservation; revoke() carries a code comment recording the Slice-6.4 forward constraint (must reject once a VendorPaymentAllocation references its lines) for the future, not enforced yet since nothing exists to check against. Project-scoping for multi-project batches is "no inaccessible line exists," not "at least one line matches," via a query-level NOT filter for list and an assertBillsProjectAccessible helper (throws NOT_FOUND, never partial exposure) for single-record operations. Five new permissions with a deliberately stricter default matrix than Purchase Request -- Employee gets no grant at all (unlike Purchase Request's read+create). New shared apps/api/src/common/sequential-number.util.ts, with PurchaseRequestsService.generateRequestNumber mechanically refactored to a two-line wrapper delegating to it (behavior-identical). Root-caused and fixed the vitest full-suite connection-pool timeout pattern (disclosed in ADR-075/076/077's own Known issues) with bounded thread pooling (poolOptions.threads.maxThreads: 4) in vitest.integration.config.ts, rather than relying on a rerun.
Frontend: New /payment-requests (list, filters incl. REVOKED status), /payment-requests/new (Legal Entity auto-select-if-one/searchable-if-many, eligible-bills checklist with per-row editable requested amount defaulting to full availability, live server preview total, Save-as-Draft/Submit two-button pattern, optional ?vendorBillId= seed resolving vendor+legal entity entirely server-side), /payment-requests/[id] (DraftEditor vs. a decided/submitted view with Approve/Reject/Cancel/Revoke actions gated by permission and self-approval state, full decision history). Vendor Bill's own [id] detail page gains a "Payment Requests" card, its "Raise Payment Request..." action shown only while a balance actually remains, not merely once FINALIZED. payment-requests/shared.tsx imports PRIORITY_OPTIONS/newIdempotencyKey from purchase-requests/shared.tsx rather than duplicating them, since Payment Request reuses the identical PurchaseRequestPriority enum.
Files changed: packages/database/prisma/schema.prisma, new migration packages/database/prisma/migrations/20260821110000_phase6_03_payment_request, packages/database/prisma/seed.ts (5 permissions, role grants, one entity type). apps/api/src/common/sequential-number.util.ts (new), apps/api/src/purchase-requests/purchase-requests.service.ts (mechanical refactor). apps/api/src/payment-requests/{payment-requests.service.ts, .module.ts, .integration-spec.ts} (new), apps/api/src/{app.module.ts, main.ts}, apps/api/src/rbac/roles.integration-spec.ts (catalog count 85 -> 90). packages/contracts/src/{ports/payment-request.port.ts, routers/payment-requests.ts} (new), wired into context.ts/router.ts/ports/index.ts/index.ts. apps/web/src/app/(platform)/payment-requests/{shared.tsx, page.tsx, new/page.tsx, [id]/page.tsx} (new), apps/web/src/app/(platform)/vendor-bills/[id]/page.tsx (Payment Requests card), apps/web/src/components/documents-tab.tsx (PAYMENT_REQUEST entity type), packages/ui/src/components/{icons.tsx, nav-items.ts}. apps/api/vitest.integration.config.ts (bounded thread pooling).
Migrations: phase6_03_payment_request -- hand-curated from `prisma migrate diff` (the same three unrelated pre-existing constraints/indexes always excluded), applied via prisma migrate deploy + prisma generate. Adds two enums, three tables, and a new uq_vendor_bills_id_org composite-unique support constraint on VendorBill (required for PaymentRequestLine's own org-consistent FK into it -- the first model needing one; mirrors the identical existing pattern on Party/LegalEntity/BankAccount/DocumentCategory).
Tests: 26 new tests in payment-requests.integration-spec.ts covering the full matrix named in the review -- multi-bill same-vendor/legal-entity batches; cross-vendor/cross-legal-entity/duplicate-bill/non-finalized/zero-amount/exceeds-available rejections; a genuine concurrent-submission race using Promise.allSettled (exactly one of two racing submits succeeds, the loser gets a clean validation error); reservation release after reject/cancel/revoke; self-approval permission + mandatory justification; PM access across every project in a batch and rejection of a mixed-project batch with no partial leakage; stale-version conflicts; idempotent replay; cross-org isolation; full decision/audit history. roles.integration-spec.ts catalog-count assertion corrected 85 -> 90. Two real bugs caught only by actually running the tests, both fixed: a `uuid = text` Postgres operator error in submit's row-locking raw SQL (fixed by casting the column, `id::text`, not the parameter); and a test's own incorrect message-substring assertion (rewritten to check the underlying NOT_FOUND error code directly). turbo run typecheck lint build clean across all 9 workspaces (27/27 tasks); apps/web production build registers all three new routes. A fresh-database migration replay against a disposable scratch database (zitaware_crm_scratch_verify, dropped after use) confirmed the full migration history -- including this slice's new migration -- applies cleanly from zero, with `prisma migrate diff` against the result showing only the same three pre-existing hand-added constraints every prior migration has excluded. The full apps/api integration suite's first clean run surfaced one real fixture defect this slice introduced (not a flake): payment-requests.integration-spec.ts reused the exact literal GSTIN already hardcoded in vendor-bills.integration-spec.ts's own cross-org fixture, which a real, nationally-unique GSTIN constraint correctly rejected as a duplicate once bounded thread concurrency let both files run at the same time -- fixed by giving the new spec file its own unique literal; the suite then passed 29/29 files, 354/354 tests clean on the next run, satisfying the review's own "first run, not a diagnostic rerun" acceptance bar.
Decisions added/changed: ADR-078 (gross-liability semantics excluding TDS; row-locked concurrency-safe reservation as the sole authoritative gate; the new REVOKED status/lifecycle and its Slice-6.4 forward constraint; the stricter-than-Purchase-Request permission matrix; "no inaccessible line exists" project-scoping; the shared sequential-number-generator extraction; the vitest connection-pool root-cause fix; the dropped title/description fields in favor of requestedPaymentDate; the server-side-only vendor/legal-entity resolution for a seeded request).
Known issues: none besides the outstanding live-browser-verification backlog noted in Status above. Slice 6.4 (Vendor Payment) is the next slice, not started -- whether TDS is always applicable and exactly when it is recognized remains an open policy question to confirm during that slice's own planning, not an assumption carried over from this one.
Next task: Slice 6.4 -- Vendor Payment (VendorPayment/VendorPaymentAllocation mirroring ClientReceipt/ReceiptAllocation, each allocation referencing a PaymentRequestLine, posting Dr Accounts Payable / Cr Bank / Cr TDS Payable) is the natural next step in the procurement chain -- needs its own fresh plan-mode pass given the open TDS policy question and the fact that no TDS rate/section master exists anywhere in this codebase yet.
```

## Phase 6, Slice 6.4: merge Purchase Request into Vendor Bill

```text
Date: 2026-08-22
Status: Implementation complete, UI verification pending -- backend fully rewritten and tested (vendor-bills.integration-spec.ts rewritten, 38/38 passing; payment-requests.integration-spec.ts fixtures fixed, 26/26 passing, confirming Payment Request's own production code needed zero changes), typecheck/lint/build clean across all 9 workspaces, a fresh-database migration replay confirmed both new migrations apply cleanly from zero, and the full apps/api integration suite passed 29/29 files clean; the frontend has not yet had a real browser pass. Do not mark this slice's UI as done until a browser pass confirms: (1) the New Vendor Bill page's Vendor/Project/Priority fields and legal-entity-derived GST registration selection work correctly, live preview included; (2) the Draft editor's same fields plus Submit/Cancel modals behave correctly; (3) Approve/Reject/Cancel/Void actions on a submitted/finalized/decided bill show and hide correctly per permission and self-approval state; (4) the Vendor Bill list/detail pages no longer show any Purchase-Request-era UI.
Phase: Phase 6 -- Procurement and expense (Slice 6.4 -- a restructuring of 6.1/6.2 rather than a new vertical, prompted directly by the user's own workflow complaint).
Task: User reported the shipped chain (Purchase Request -> Vendor Bill -> Payment Request -> Vendor Payment) was too long for day-to-day use -- an employee needed a pre-approved estimate before a real vendor invoice even existed, then a separate Vendor Bill had to be raised against that approval before payment could even be requested. Reading the three original HTML prototypes this flow was derived from (purchase-request.html/purchase-view.html/purchase-payment.html) confirmed they never actually specified a two-document request+bill split -- they show one request (with the real invoice attached), one view/edit screen, and one "mark as paid" screen. The user proposed merging Purchase Request's fields directly into Vendor Bill: Vendor Bill (with all details and actual GST amounts) -> Payment Request -> Vendor Payment. AskUserQuestion confirmed three directional decisions before planning: the approval gate moves from "before purchase" to "after the vendor bill exists"; the Purchase Request module is deleted entirely, not kept as unused code; Approve and Finalize become one combined action. Research (two parallel Explore passes) confirmed a clean merge -- Payment Request and the not-yet-built Vendor Payment never reach through Vendor Bill into Purchase Request for anything. The resulting plan was approved with no revisions. Full rationale in docs/12 ADR-079.
Backend: VendorBill's state machine expands to DRAFT -> SUBMITTED -> FINALIZED (approve(), renamed from finalize() -- combines the maker-checker decision ported verbatim from the deleted PurchaseRequestsService.approve() with the identical journal posting finalize() always did, both in one transaction with a new VendorBillDecision row) / REJECTED; DRAFT/SUBMITTED -> CANCELLED (creator-only, reason required -- replaces the old "Void Draft" flip); FINALIZED -> VOID (unchanged). FINALIZED itself is an unchanged enum value, which is exactly why Payment Request and the future Vendor Payment slice needed zero code changes. vendorId/projectId/priority are now entered directly (legalEntityId derived from the selected project); purchaseRequestId, purchaseRequestEstimatedAmount, cumulativeBilledAmount, and the varianceTone "exceeds estimate" UI are all removed -- none were ever persisted columns beyond purchaseRequestId itself. PurchaseRequestPriority renamed (not dropped) to VendorBillPriority via ALTER TYPE, since PaymentRequest.priority already depended on it. New VendorBillDecision table mirrors the deleted PurchaseRequestDecision exactly. Permissions collapse from 9 combined keys to 5 (vendor_bill.{read,create,approve,self_approve,void}); Project Manager gains vendor_bill.approve and Employee gains vendor_bill.{read,create} as necessary parity fixes. The seed script's permission-upsert loop was additive-only by design (never previously needed to remove a permission) -- added a stale-permission/stale-EntityType prune step ahead of the upsert loop so the catalog always reflects exactly what PERMISSION_KEYS declares. purchase-requests module deleted entirely (service, module, integration-spec, contracts port/router), unwired from app.module.ts/main.ts/context.ts/router.ts.
Frontend: apps/web/src/app/(platform)/purchase-requests/ deleted entirely. vendor-bills/{shared.tsx, page.tsx, new/page.tsx, [id]/page.tsx} rewritten to absorb Purchase Request's own field set (Vendor/Project/Priority selects, legal-entity-derived GST registration) and its maker-checker Approve/Reject/Cancel modal UI (hidden-not-disabled self-approval-gated Approve button, mandatory-note self-approval modal, Approval History list with the "Self-approved" pill) -- merged with Vendor Bill's own existing line-item table, GST breakdown, Void action, and PaymentRequestsCard. payment-requests/shared.tsx's PRIORITY_OPTIONS/newIdempotencyKey import switched from the deleted purchase-requests/shared to vendor-bills/shared. documents-tab.tsx drops the PURCHASE_REQUEST entity type. packages/ui/src/components/{icons.tsx, nav-items.ts, index.ts} drop PurchaseRequestIcon and its nav entry.
Files changed: packages/database/prisma/schema.prisma, two new migrations (packages/database/prisma/migrations/{20260822090000_phase6_04_merge_purchase_request_into_vendor_bill, 20260822093000_phase6_04b_vendor_bill_number_reuse_after_cancel}), packages/database/prisma/seed.ts. apps/api/src/vendor-bills/vendor-bills.service.ts (rewritten), apps/api/src/purchase-requests/ (deleted), apps/api/src/{app.module.ts, main.ts}, apps/api/src/common/sequential-number.util.ts (doc comment), apps/api/src/rbac/roles.integration-spec.ts (catalog count 90 -> 86), apps/api/src/vendor-bills/vendor-bills.integration-spec.ts (rewritten), apps/api/src/payment-requests/payment-requests.integration-spec.ts (fixtures fixed). packages/contracts/src/ports/vendor-bill.port.ts and routers/vendor-bills.ts (rewritten), packages/contracts/src/{ports/purchase-request.port.ts, routers/purchase-requests.ts} (deleted), wiring updated in context.ts/router.ts/ports/index.ts/index.ts. apps/web/src/app/(platform)/purchase-requests/ (deleted), apps/web/src/app/(platform)/vendor-bills/{shared.tsx, page.tsx, new/page.tsx, [id]/page.tsx} (rewritten), apps/web/src/app/(platform)/payment-requests/shared.tsx (import switch), apps/web/src/components/documents-tab.tsx, packages/ui/src/components/{icons.tsx, nav-items.ts, index.ts}.
Migrations: phase6_04_merge_purchase_request_into_vendor_bill (the main merge -- drops purchase_requests/purchase_request_lines/purchase_request_decisions and three of their four enums, renames PurchaseRequestPriority via ALTER TYPE rather than drop-and-recreate to avoid destroying PaymentRequest.priority data, adds the new vendor_bills columns and status values, creates vendor_bill_decisions) and phase6_04b_vendor_bill_number_reuse_after_cancel (a follow-up hand-written fix, not diff-generated -- corrects uq_vendor_bills_vendor_number's WHERE clause from status != 'VOID' to NOT IN ('VOID','CANCELLED'), since a CANCELLED bill, like a VOID one, never posted anything and should free up its own vendor-bill-number for reuse; caught while writing the new test suite, not found separately). Both applied via prisma migrate deploy; a fresh-database replay on a disposable scratch database confirmed the full history applies cleanly from zero.
Tests: vendor-bills.integration-spec.ts fully rewritten (38 tests) covering both the pre-existing GST/ITC/ledger-grouping/void-reversal coverage and the newly-absorbed maker-checker matrix (self-approval permission + mandatory justification, creator-cannot-approve-without-permission, reject terminal, cancel creator-only + reason reachable from DRAFT and SUBMITTED, approve not retroactively blocked by a since-cancelled project while create/submit are, idempotent replay on every mutating command, version conflicts, vendor-bill-number reuse after cancel or void) -- 38/38 passing. payment-requests.integration-spec.ts's createFinalizedBill fixture helper rewritten to build directly through Vendor Bill's own draft->submit->approve flow (Payment Request's own production code needed zero changes) -- 26/26 passing. roles.integration-spec.ts catalog count corrected 90 -> 86, verified against the live seeded database directly before the test itself confirmed it -- 7/7 passing. turbo run typecheck lint build clean across all 9 workspaces. Full apps/api integration suite: 29/29 files clean on a fresh run.
Decisions added/changed: ADR-079 (the state-machine merge and why FINALIZED staying unchanged meant zero Payment Request changes; the REVOKED-style VendorBillDecision table; the PurchaseRequestPriority rename-not-drop; the permission-matrix collapse and its two necessary parity grants; the vendor-bill-number-reuse-after-cancel fix). Supersedes ADR-074 (Purchase Request's own design, now deleted). Amends ADR-075/076 (Vendor Bill's own design -- state machine, permissions, and header fields changed; the accounting posting logic itself is untouched).
Known issues: none besides the outstanding live-browser-verification backlog noted in Status above. Vendor Payment is the next slice (renumbered 6.5, since this merge occupies 6.4) -- still not started; the open TDS-policy question from ADR-078 carries forward unchanged.
Next task: Slice 6.5 -- Vendor Payment (VendorPayment/VendorPaymentAllocation mirroring ClientReceipt/ReceiptAllocation, each allocation referencing a PaymentRequestLine, posting Dr Accounts Payable / Cr Bank / Cr TDS Payable) is the natural next step in the procurement chain -- needs its own fresh plan-mode pass given the open TDS policy question and the fact that no TDS rate/section master exists anywhere in this codebase yet.
